# SSS-L3 — 62 historical public properties

These records come from the immutable public predecessor, before the separately accepted current subject. Their historical identity and status are preserved. They are not a publication of private current qualification interpretations. A property reader is a way to inspect the complete public evidence, not an adoption checklist.

## ESPLE-001 — Warranted family boundary

Historical source pointer: `/properties/0` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json

  "FORMULATION": "Program-family reasoning and enterprise-scoped domain engineering, rather than a claim that any code similarity defines a family.",

    "ESPLE-S009": "2005"
  }

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Program-family reasoning and enterprise-scoped domain engineering, rather than a claim that any code similarity defines a family.

### Problem it addressed

Superficially similar products can require incompatible assumptions, release obligations or ownership arrangements.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Superficially similar products can require incompatible assumptions, release obligations or ownership arrangements.

### Mechanism

State which products and foreseeable needs belong together; test shared domain assumptions against counterexamples before committing to shared production.

### Trigger or context

A decision to coordinate assets or future variants across more than one product need.

### Non trigger or cheap path

For incidental shared utilities, use ordinary component reuse or independent products without introducing a family programme.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Product needs, differences, expected future use and who can sustain shared assets.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "The family consists of products whose shared domain assumptions warrant coordinated production; code resemblance alone is insufficient.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Commonality must survive comparison of actual needs; variation includes intended alternatives, not every historical difference.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features are statements about product needs before they become selectable variables; scope precedes formal completeness.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "No binding time is prescribed by scoping; the cost and feasibility of possible binding choices constrain what the family can promise.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Only assets with a defensible cross-product consumer belong in the shared investment case.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Scope identifies which derived products the family claims to support; precise baseline identity is established by the production model.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Broader scope can add interactions; scoping alone supplies no coverage or correctness result.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changed demand or incompatible product assumptions can require a split, migration or end of shared production.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Affected product stakeholders must be represented; legitimate exceptions can invalidate a too-broad common-core assumption.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Expected reuse, coordination and support cost must be compared with independent products or selective component reuse.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P1",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P1",
    "TRIGGER": "A decision to coordinate assets or future variants across more than one product need.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Product needs, differences, expected future use and who can sustain shared assets.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "State which products and foreseeable needs belong together; test shared domain assumptions against counterexamples before committing to shared production.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Included and excluded products have reasons; at least one plausible contrary case has been adjudicated; the chosen boundary supports feasible derivation.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "For incidental shared utilities, use ordinary component reuse or independent products without introducing a family programme.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Reopen the boundary when required assumptions diverge; dissolve or split the family when coordinated reuse no longer earns its cost."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Superficially similar products can require incompatible assumptions, release obligations or ownership arrangements. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Portfolio and domain decision-makers with product representatives; similarity analysis does not authorise merging teams or obligations.

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio and domain decision-makers with product representatives; similarity analysis does not authorise merging teams or obligations. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Product needs, differences, expected future use and who can sustain shared assets.; the result must expose Included and excluded products have reasons; at least one plausible contrary case has been adjudicated; the chosen boundary supports feasible derivation. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Included and excluded products have reasons; at least one plausible contrary case has been adjudicated; the chosen boundary supports feasible derivation.

### Known failure modes

```json
  "Economic and market assumptions can be wrong even when the family model is internally precise. Bosch's mixed dependencies also warn against forcing all products into one homogeneous model.",
  "Scoping and negotiation consume effort before a reusable asset exists."
]
```

### Disposition rationale

The bounded invariant survives the examined criticisms; this is not a universal estimate of economic benefit or a requirement for a particular tool. Economic and market assumptions can be wrong even when the family model is internally precise. Bosch's mixed dependencies also warn against forcing all products into one homogeneous model.

## ESPLE-002 — Evidence of commonality and variability

Historical source pointer: `/properties/1` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Domain analysis draws on existing systems, domain experts and other information sources; commonality analysis makes reusable knowledge explicit.",

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Domain analysis draws on existing systems, domain experts and other information sources; commonality analysis makes reusable knowledge explicit.

### Problem it addressed

Duplication can be accidental, while valuable commonality may not yet exist as duplicated code.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Duplication can be accidental, while valuable commonality may not yet exist as duplicated code.

### Mechanism

Compare requirements, products and expert explanations; separate invariant needs, intended variation and historical accidents; record disagreement rather than infer a common core from names.

### Trigger or context

Establishing or revising the domain knowledge on which reusable assets depend.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A short reviewed comparison of a few stable products can replace an elaborate modelling exercise.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Representative products, stakeholder needs, operational differences and access to domain expertise.

### Domain profile

```json
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features are statements about product needs before they become selectable variables; scope precedes formal completeness.",

  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P1",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P1",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Duplication can be accidental, while valuable commonality may not yet exist as duplicated code. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Domain engineers and product experts exchange examples and exceptions; neither an extractor nor one expert unilaterally determines all valid products.

### Actor and authority boundary

Domain engineers and product experts exchange examples and exceptions; neither an extractor nor one expert unilaterally determines all valid products. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Representative products, stakeholder needs, operational differences and access to domain expertise.; the result must expose Claimed commonalities have examples and scope; observed differences have an explicit disposition rather than automatically becoming features. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Claimed commonalities have examples and scope; observed differences have an explicit disposition rather than automatically becoming features.

### Known failure modes

```json
  "Existing products are a selected historical sample, not a complete or necessarily desirable domain. Reverse-engineered constraints can miss meaningful combinations.",
  "Interviews, comparison and maintenance of domain knowledge; disagreement may delay derivation."
]
```

## ESPLE-003 — Value-bound admission of variability

Historical source pointer: `/properties/2` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Product-focused scoping and managed variability restrict investment to anticipated family needs.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S007",
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S012"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S007": "1999",
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S012": "2005-02"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Product-focused scoping and managed variability restrict investment to anticipated family needs.

### Problem it addressed

Every possible option adds interactions and maintenance without necessarily serving a product.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Every possible option adds interactions and maintenance without necessarily serving a product.

### Mechanism

Admit a new variation only with a product or credible future need, an implementation boundary, an assurance consequence and an identified support cost.

### Trigger or context

A request to broaden the set of products or selectable behaviours.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep a one-off requirement product-local, or postpone its generalisation until repeated demand supplies evidence.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Demand rationale, expected reuse, interaction impact and costs of both local and shared realisation.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "The family consists of products whose shared domain assumptions warrant coordinated production; code resemblance alone is insufficient.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Commonality must survive comparison of actual needs; variation includes intended alternatives, not every historical difference.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features are statements about product needs before they become selectable variables; scope precedes formal completeness.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "No binding time is prescribed by scoping; the cost and feasibility of possible binding choices constrain what the family can promise.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Only assets with a defensible cross-product consumer belong in the shared investment case.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Scope identifies which derived products the family claims to support; precise baseline identity is established by the production model.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Broader scope can add interactions; scoping alone supplies no coverage or correctness result.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changed demand or incompatible product assumptions can require a split, migration or end of shared production.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Affected product stakeholders must be represented; legitimate exceptions can invalidate a too-broad common-core assumption.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Expected reuse, coordination and support cost must be compared with independent products or selective component reuse.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P1",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P1",
    "TRIGGER": "A request to broaden the set of products or selectable behaviours.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Demand rationale, expected reuse, interaction impact and costs of both local and shared realisation.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Admit a new variation only with a product or credible future need, an implementation boundary, an assurance consequence and an identified support cost.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "An admitted option has a consumer and support path; rejected or deferred options retain their rationale.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Keep a one-off requirement product-local, or postpone its generalisation until repeated demand supplies evidence.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Reassess the option when its demand disappears; support-aware removal is a separate obligation in ESPLE-061."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Every possible option adds interactions and maintenance without necessarily serving a product. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product sponsors and shared-asset maintainers negotiate the scope; this criterion does not require a new board for each feature.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product sponsors and shared-asset maintainers negotiate the scope; this criterion does not require a new board for each feature. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Demand rationale, expected reuse, interaction impact and costs of both local and shared realisation.; the result must expose An admitted option has a consumer and support path; rejected or deferred options retain their rationale. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

An admitted option has a consumer and support path; rejected or deferred options retain their rationale.

### Known failure modes

```json
  "Overly restrictive admission can suppress valuable experimentation; speculative demand can also be used to justify permanent complexity.",
  "Decision latency and potential lost opportunities from deferral."
]
```

## ESPLE-004 — Coupled domain and application responsibilities

Historical source pointer: `/properties/3` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "FAST and major SPLE frameworks distinguish domain assets from application derivation while coupling their work.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S006",
    "ESPLE-S008",
    "ESPLE-S009"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S006": "2000",
    "ESPLE-S008": "2001 publication; commonly cited 2002",
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

FAST and major SPLE frameworks distinguish domain assets from application derivation while coupling their work.

### Problem it addressed

A reusable platform can become detached from products, or product teams can silently duplicate domain responsibility.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A reusable platform can become detached from products, or product teams can silently duplicate domain responsibility.

### Mechanism

Maintain a functional distinction between creating family capabilities and instantiating products, with explicit exchange of requirements, constraints and feasibility evidence.

### Trigger or context

At least one asset or production decision is intended to serve several products.

### Non trigger or cheap path

The same people may perform both activities; a small family needs no separate departments or serial phases.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Defined family scope, asset responsibilities and actual product requests.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Domain and application work are justified by a family whose reusable capabilities serve actual or credible products.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "The common core and product-local work are related but not identical; repeated local differences require examination, not automatic generalisation.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Product requests can expose absent or mistaken features and constraints; feedback may change the model or preserve an exception.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Shared and local decisions can bind at different stages; adoption strategy must not predetermine all technical binding choices.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Domain work maintains reusable assets; application work derives products and returns feasibility and failure evidence.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product observations need enough baseline and configuration identity to be actionable across the family.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Local success does not prove safe generalisation; shared repairs need product-impact analysis.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Feedback is recurrent and can interrupt planned development; extractive, reactive and proactive routes can coexist over time.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "The same people may hold both responsibilities; exceptions retain support accountability and limits on inherited guarantees.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination and asset preparation are real costs; a small shared capability or continued cloning can dominate full platform adoption.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P2",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P2",
    "TRIGGER": "At least one asset or production decision is intended to serve several products.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Defined family scope, asset responsibilities and actual product requests.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Maintain a functional distinction between creating family capabilities and instantiating products, with explicit exchange of requirements, constraints and feasibility evidence.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Product work can identify the reusable baseline it consumes, and asset work can identify the products it supports.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "The same people may perform both activities; a small family needs no separate departments or serial phases.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "If no cross-product asset remains, use a single-product process rather than preserve two ceremonial tracks."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A reusable platform can become detached from products, or product teams can silently duplicate domain responsibility. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Asset developers and product developers need reciprocal access to decisions; management supplies resources rather than treating a model as authority.

### Actor and authority boundary

Asset developers and product developers need reciprocal access to decisions; management supplies resources rather than treating a model as authority. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Defined family scope, asset responsibilities and actual product requests.; the result must expose Product work can identify the reusable baseline it consumes, and asset work can identify the products it supports. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Product work can identify the reusable baseline it consumes, and asset work can identify the products it supports.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Organisational separation can become a bottleneck or produce assets nobody uses. The distinction is about responsibilities, not a mandatory team topology.",
  "Coordination and reconciliation of competing release schedules."
]
```

## ESPLE-005 — Product-to-core feedback

Historical source pointer: `/properties/4` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Application experience feeds domain requirements, assets and production processes in iterative product-line frameworks.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S006",
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S011"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S006": "2000",
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Application experience feeds domain requirements, assets and production processes in iterative product-line frameworks.

### Problem it addressed

Defects, workarounds and new needs stay local while the shared platform continues producing the same mismatch.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Defects, workarounds and new needs stay local while the shared platform continues producing the same mismatch.

### Mechanism

Return a product observation with its configuration, asset baseline and consequence; decide whether to repair the core, adjust a constraint, retain an exception or revise scope.

### Trigger or context

Derivation failure, recurring local workaround, unexpected interaction or a justified new common need.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A direct conversation and linked change record can suffice when the team and affected products are small.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Reproducible observation, affected product context and a maintainer able to change the relevant shared asset.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Domain and application work are justified by a family whose reusable capabilities serve actual or credible products.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "The common core and product-local work are related but not identical; repeated local differences require examination, not automatic generalisation.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Product requests can expose absent or mistaken features and constraints; feedback may change the model or preserve an exception.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Shared and local decisions can bind at different stages; adoption strategy must not predetermine all technical binding choices.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Domain work maintains reusable assets; application work derives products and returns feasibility and failure evidence.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product observations need enough baseline and configuration identity to be actionable across the family.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Local success does not prove safe generalisation; shared repairs need product-impact analysis.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Feedback is recurrent and can interrupt planned development; extractive, reactive and proactive routes can coexist over time.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "The same people may hold both responsibilities; exceptions retain support accountability and limits on inherited guarantees.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination and asset preparation are real costs; a small shared capability or continued cloning can dominate full platform adoption.",

    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Return a product observation with its configuration, asset baseline and consequence; decide whether to repair the core, adjust a constraint, retain an exception or revise scope.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Feedback has a reasoned disposition and the producing product receives the result; absence of a core change can be legitimate.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A direct conversation and linked change record can suffice when the team and affected products are small.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Stop recurring review when the triggering problem is resolved or outside the family; retain the decision's trace for supported versions."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Defects, workarounds and new needs stay local while the shared platform continues producing the same mismatch. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product engineers communicate evidence to domain maintainers; affected product owners participate when obligations change.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product engineers communicate evidence to domain maintainers; affected product owners participate when obligations change. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Reproducible observation, affected product context and a maintainer able to change the relevant shared asset.; the result must expose Feedback has a reasoned disposition and the producing product receives the result; absence of a core change can be legitimate. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Feedback has a reasoned disposition and the producing product receives the result; absence of a core change can be legitimate.

```json
]
```

## ESPLE-006 — Product exceptions with retained lineage

Historical source pointer: `/properties/5` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Extractive/reactive adoption and industrial mixed-platform practice allow product-specific differences rather than requiring one universal core.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S010",
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S010": "2001 workshop; 2002 proceedings",
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Extractive/reactive adoption and industrial mixed-platform practice allow product-specific differences rather than requiring one universal core.

### Problem it addressed

Either all exceptions are forbidden, or local forks erase the relationship needed for fixes and future extraction.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Either all exceptions are forbidden, or local forks erase the relationship needed for fixes and future extraction.

### Mechanism

Permit a bounded product divergence with its baseline, purpose, support responsibility and conditions for reintegration or continued separation.

### Trigger or context

A product obligation cannot be met adequately through the current shared assets without disproportionate disruption.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A small isolated patch with a recorded origin may be better than a new family-wide variation point.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Product obligation, impact on other products and an owner willing to maintain the exception.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Domain and application work are justified by a family whose reusable capabilities serve actual or credible products.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "The common core and product-local work are related but not identical; repeated local differences require examination, not automatic generalisation.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Product requests can expose absent or mistaken features and constraints; feedback may change the model or preserve an exception.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Shared and local decisions can bind at different stages; adoption strategy must not predetermine all technical binding choices.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Domain work maintains reusable assets; application work derives products and returns feasibility and failure evidence.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product observations need enough baseline and configuration identity to be actionable across the family.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Local success does not prove safe generalisation; shared repairs need product-impact analysis.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Feedback is recurrent and can interrupt planned development; extractive, reactive and proactive routes can coexist over time.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "The same people may hold both responsibilities; exceptions retain support accountability and limits on inherited guarantees.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination and asset preparation are real costs; a small shared capability or continued cloning can dominate full platform adoption.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P2",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P2",
    "TRIGGER": "A product obligation cannot be met adequately through the current shared assets without disproportionate disruption.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Product obligation, impact on other products and an owner willing to maintain the exception.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Permit a bounded product divergence with its baseline, purpose, support responsibility and conditions for reintegration or continued separation.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "The exceptional product is identifiable, its unsupported shared claims are withdrawn, and applicable fixes can be located.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A small isolated patch with a recorded origin may be better than a new family-wide variation point.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Reintegrate when a genuine common mechanism emerges, or remove family membership when divergence is enduring and independent."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Either all exceptions are forbidden, or local forks erase the relationship needed for fixes and future extraction. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product and core maintainers agree who carries future changes; permission to diverge is not permission to misrepresent family assurance.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product and core maintainers agree who carries future changes; permission to diverge is not permission to misrepresent family assurance. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Product obligation, impact on other products and an owner willing to maintain the exception.; the result must expose The exceptional product is identifiable, its unsupported shared claims are withdrawn, and applicable fixes can be located. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The exceptional product is identifiable, its unsupported shared claims are withdrawn, and applicable fixes can be located.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Exception registries can merely legitimise permanent drift. Conversely, mandatory reintegration may recreate the original incompatibility.",
  "Parallel maintenance, merge conflicts and reduced family-wide assurance reuse."
]
```

## ESPLE-007 — Selection among proactive, reactive and extractive adoption

Historical source pointer: `/properties/6` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Proactive, reactive and extractive adoption describe different starting conditions and can be combined.",

    "ESPLE-S007",
    "ESPLE-S010"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",

    "ESPLE-S007": "1999",
    "ESPLE-S010": "2001 workshop; 2002 proceedings"
  }

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Proactive, reactive and extractive adoption describe different starting conditions and can be combined.

### Problem it addressed

A compulsory large upfront platform investment can outrun demand or discard working products.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A compulsory large upfront platform investment can outrun demand or discard working products.

### Mechanism

Choose anticipatory domain investment, incremental extension or recovery from existing variants according to uncertainty, existing assets and available migration capacity.

### Trigger or context

Starting a product line or changing how an existing portfolio is produced.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Reuse a few stable assets or retain clone-and-own while collecting evidence; no immediate platform conversion is required.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Demand horizon, asset state, change rate, budget and the cost of delaying products.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Domain and application work are justified by a family whose reusable capabilities serve actual or credible products.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "The common core and product-local work are related but not identical; repeated local differences require examination, not automatic generalisation.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Product requests can expose absent or mistaken features and constraints; feedback may change the model or preserve an exception.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Shared and local decisions can bind at different stages; adoption strategy must not predetermine all technical binding choices.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Domain work maintains reusable assets; application work derives products and returns feasibility and failure evidence.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product observations need enough baseline and configuration identity to be actionable across the family.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Local success does not prove safe generalisation; shared repairs need product-impact analysis.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Feedback is recurrent and can interrupt planned development; extractive, reactive and proactive routes can coexist over time.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "The same people may hold both responsibilities; exceptions retain support accountability and limits on inherited guarantees.",

    "FAMILY": "P2",
    "TRIGGER": "Starting a product line or changing how an existing portfolio is produced.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Demand horizon, asset state, change rate, budget and the cost of delaying products.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Choose anticipatory domain investment, incremental extension or recovery from existing variants according to uncertainty, existing assets and available migration capacity.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "The chosen route states what is invested now, what remains product-local and what evidence triggers further consolidation.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Reuse a few stable assets or retain clone-and-own while collecting evidence; no immediate platform conversion is required.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Change route when uncertainty resolves, stable commonality emerges or expected reuse fails to materialise."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A compulsory large upfront platform investment can outrun demand or discard working products. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Portfolio sponsors and engineering leads select a transition strategy; a tool supplier's capability does not determine organisational readiness.

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio sponsors and engineering leads select a transition strategy; a tool supplier's capability does not determine organisational readiness. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Demand horizon, asset state, change rate, budget and the cost of delaying products.; the result must expose The chosen route states what is invested now, what remains product-local and what evidence triggers further consolidation. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The chosen route states what is invested now, what remains product-local and what evidence triggers further consolidation.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Reactive growth can accumulate hidden debt, extraction can preserve accidental variation, and proactive design can overfit forecasts.",
  "Each route distributes investment, interruption and future rework differently."
]
```

## ESPLE-008 — Explicit feature semantics

Historical source pointer: `/properties/7` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Feature modelling organises domain-relevant characteristics, but implementation variables and stakeholder features need not coincide.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S005",
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S014"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S005": "1990-11",
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S014": "2005"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Feature modelling organises domain-relevant characteristics, but implementation variables and stakeholder features need not coincide.

### Problem it addressed

The same word is treated as a requirement, a module and a selectable flag despite different meanings.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

The same word is treated as a requirement, a module and a selectable flag despite different meanings.

### Mechanism

Define what each feature denotes, who interprets it and how selections relate to requirements, implementation and runtime capability.

### Trigger or context

Features are used to negotiate products, derive artefacts or justify assurance.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A compact glossary plus explicit configuration rules can be sufficient; a tree is optional.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Stakeholder vocabulary, variation decisions and the relevant implementation mapping.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "The model represents a declared product family, not every conceivable configuration in the technology domain.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common and variable characteristics are explicit semantic decisions; historical product observations do not exhaust valid possibilities.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Boolean, cardinality, attribute and typed constraints describe encoded validity; domain adequacy must be checked outside solver consistency.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "A model choice may bind before build, at deployment or at runtime; selection semantics do not determine realisation timing by themselves.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Features relate to requirements and assets through possibly many-to-many mappings; neither a node nor a flag is automatically a module.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "A configuration identifies model selections under a particular model version; it is not yet the identity of a built product.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Constraint analysis excludes some invalid combinations but does not establish implementation interactions or input coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model edits can add or remove products; feature correspondence and changed meaning must be considered during migration.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Experts validate domain intent and configurators make product decisions within authorised bounds; models can be decentralised if composition is checked.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Use the least costly representation that preserves consequential distinctions; a decision table or direct guidance can be adequate.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P3",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P3",
    "TRIGGER": "Features are used to negotiate products, derive artefacts or justify assurance.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Stakeholder vocabulary, variation decisions and the relevant implementation mapping.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Define what each feature denotes, who interprets it and how selections relate to requirements, implementation and runtime capability.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A selected feature has a checkable meaning at the product boundary, including attributes or multiplicity where required.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A compact glossary plus explicit configuration rules can be sufficient; a tree is optional.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove semantic entries only with the associated supported feature or replace them with an explicit migration mapping."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: The same word is treated as a requirement, a module and a selectable flag despite different meanings. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Domain experts, configurators and developers must resolve semantic disagreement; the modeller cannot decide stakeholder intent by naming a node.

### Actor and authority boundary

Domain experts, configurators and developers must resolve semantic disagreement; the modeller cannot decide stakeholder intent by naming a node. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Stakeholder vocabulary, variation decisions and the relevant implementation mapping.; the result must expose A selected feature has a checkable meaning at the product boundary, including attributes or multiplicity where required. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A selected feature has a checkable meaning at the product boundary, including attributes or multiplicity where required.

## ESPLE-009 — Constraint-consistency diagnostics

Historical source pointer: `/properties/8` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Propositional interpretations enable detection of void models, dead features and unintentionally forced options.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S014",
    "ESPLE-S015"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S014": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S015": "2010"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Propositional interpretations enable detection of void models, dead features and unintentionally forced options.

### Problem it addressed

Encoded constraints contradict one another or make purported choices impossible.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Encoded constraints contradict one another or make purported choices impossible.

### Mechanism

Check the model's declared semantics for consistency and investigate diagnoses relative to intended configurations; report the exact model and solver interpretation.

### Trigger or context

Constraints are nontrivial, edited, combined or used to reject user selections.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Exhaustive enumeration or a manually checked decision table may suffice for a tiny model.

### Dependencies or preconditions

A defined constraint language, faithful encoding and intended interpretations for anomalies.

### Domain profile

### Actor and authority boundary

## ESPLE-010 — Validation of domain-constraint adequacy

Historical source pointer: `/properties/9` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Domain models require knowledge beyond extracted code dependencies or propositional consistency.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S005",
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S005": "1990-11",
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Domain models require knowledge beyond extracted code dependencies or propositional consistency.

### Problem it addressed

Encoded-valid configurations can still be meaningless, unsafe or impossible in the intended domain.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Encoded-valid configurations can still be meaningless, unsafe or impossible in the intended domain.

### Mechanism

Validate constraints against representative positive and negative product scenarios, expert knowledge and implementation evidence; explicitly record unmodelled conditions.

### Trigger or context

A variability model is relied upon to accept or exclude product choices.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Review a small set of known supported and forbidden combinations instead of building a comprehensive formal ontology.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Domain obligations, known product examples, counterexamples and the boundary of extraction or modelling.

### Domain profile

```json
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "The model represents a declared product family, not every conceivable configuration in the technology domain.",

  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Boolean, cardinality, attribute and typed constraints describe encoded validity; domain adequacy must be checked outside solver consistency.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "A model choice may bind before build, at deployment or at runtime; selection semantics do not determine realisation timing by themselves.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Features relate to requirements and assets through possibly many-to-many mappings; neither a node nor a flag is automatically a module.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "A configuration identifies model selections under a particular model version; it is not yet the identity of a built product.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Constraint analysis excludes some invalid combinations but does not establish implementation interactions or input coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model edits can add or remove products; feature correspondence and changed meaning must be considered during migration.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Experts validate domain intent and configurators make product decisions within authorised bounds; models can be decentralised if composition is checked.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Use the least costly representation that preserves consequential distinctions; a decision table or direct guidance can be adequate.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P3",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P3",
    "TRIGGER": "A variability model is relied upon to accept or exclude product choices.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Domain obligations, known product examples, counterexamples and the boundary of extraction or modelling.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Validate constraints against representative positive and negative product scenarios, expert knowledge and implementation evidence; explicitly record unmodelled conditions.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Important rejected and accepted combinations have reasons traceable outside the model itself; limitations accompany configuration advice.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Review a small set of known supported and forbidden combinations instead of building a comprehensive formal ontology.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove obsolete constraints only after checking supported products and external obligations; do not equate unused with invalid."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Encoded-valid configurations can still be meaningless, unsafe or impossible in the intended domain. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Domain experts and product stakeholders validate meaning; technical extraction contributes evidence but cannot supply every missing obligation.

### Actor and authority boundary

Domain experts and product stakeholders validate meaning; technical extraction contributes evidence but cannot supply every missing obligation. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Domain obligations, known product examples, counterexamples and the boundary of extraction or modelling.; the result must expose Important rejected and accepted combinations have reasons traceable outside the model itself; limitations accompany configuration advice. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Important rejected and accepted combinations have reasons traceable outside the model itself; limitations accompany configuration advice.

## ESPLE-011 — Explanation-assisted configuration

Historical source pointer: `/properties/10` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Configuration analysis and human-oriented extraction produce explanations and comprehensible constraints, not merely yes/no answers.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S015",
    "ESPLE-S032",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S015": "2010",
    "ESPLE-S032": "2025",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Configuration analysis and human-oriented extraction produce explanations and comprehensible constraints, not merely yes/no answers.

### Problem it addressed

Users cannot repair a rejected configuration or understand why an option disappeared.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Users cannot repair a rejected configuration or understand why an option disappeared.

### Mechanism

Relate a conflict or forced choice to the smallest useful set of decisions and constraints; permit human correction and show relevant provenance.

### Trigger or context

Configuration choices are numerous, cross-constrained or made by users without implementation expertise.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A domain expert can explain a small stable set of rules directly; automated minimal explanations are not always needed.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Meaningful feature semantics, decision history and a link from the diagnosis to a possible user action.

### Domain profile

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Users cannot repair a rejected configuration or understand why an option disappeared. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Configurators and domain experts; an explanation assists choice but must not conceal alternative repairs or silently change requirements.

### Actor and authority boundary

Configurators and domain experts; an explanation assists choice but must not conceal alternative repairs or silently change requirements. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Meaningful feature semantics, decision history and a link from the diagnosis to a possible user action.; the result must expose The consumer can identify an acceptable repair or understand why no permitted product meets the request. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The consumer can identify an acceptable repair or understand why no permitted product meets the request.

## ESPLE-012 — Richer variability types when the domain requires them

Historical source pointer: `/properties/11` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Variability models extend beyond Boolean selection to attributes, cardinalities, numerical values and multiple representation levels.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S032",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S032": "2025",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Variability models extend beyond Boolean selection to attributes, cardinalities, numerical values and multiple representation levels.

### Problem it addressed

A Boolean feature tree cannot faithfully represent quantities, resource limits or value-dependent choices.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A Boolean feature tree cannot faithfully represent quantities, resource limits or value-dependent choices.

### Mechanism

Use the least expressive representation that preserves required distinctions; verify translations and tool support for the actual language level.

### Trigger or context

The domain requires multiplicity, arithmetic, typed values or constraints not expressible adequately by simple selection.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep a Boolean model or a small typed table when that already captures consequential decisions.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Value domains, intended constraints and known limitations of the chosen analysis tools.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "The model represents a declared product family, not every conceivable configuration in the technology domain.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common and variable characteristics are explicit semantic decisions; historical product observations do not exhaust valid possibilities.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Boolean, cardinality, attribute and typed constraints describe encoded validity; domain adequacy must be checked outside solver consistency.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "A model choice may bind before build, at deployment or at runtime; selection semantics do not determine realisation timing by themselves.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Features relate to requirements and assets through possibly many-to-many mappings; neither a node nor a flag is automatically a module.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "A configuration identifies model selections under a particular model version; it is not yet the identity of a built product.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Constraint analysis excludes some invalid combinations but does not establish implementation interactions or input coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model edits can add or remove products; feature correspondence and changed meaning must be considered during migration.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Experts validate domain intent and configurators make product decisions within authorised bounds; models can be decentralised if composition is checked.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Use the least costly representation that preserves consequential distinctions; a decision table or direct guidance can be adequate.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P3",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P3",
    "TRIGGER": "The domain requires multiplicity, arithmetic, typed values or constraints not expressible adequately by simple selection.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Value domains, intended constraints and known limitations of the chosen analysis tools.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Use the least expressive representation that preserves required distinctions; verify translations and tool support for the actual language level.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Required domain distinctions survive parsing, transformation and analysis, with unsupported cases explicitly rejected or handled separately.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Keep a Boolean model or a small typed table when that already captures consequential decisions.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Simplify representation when removed requirements no longer need its expressive power."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A Boolean feature tree cannot faithfully represent quantities, resource limits or value-dependent choices. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Modellers and tool integrators agree the supported semantics; unsupported constructs must not be silently ignored.

### Actor and authority boundary

Modellers and tool integrators agree the supported semantics; unsupported constructs must not be silently ignored. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Value domains, intended constraints and known limitations of the chosen analysis tools.; the result must expose Required domain distinctions survive parsing, transformation and analysis, with unsupported cases explicitly rejected or handled separately. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Required domain distinctions survive parsing, transformation and analysis, with unsupported cases explicitly rejected or handled separately.

## ESPLE-013 — Deliberate binding-time selection

Historical source pointer: `/properties/12` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Variability may be resolved during development, compilation, deployment or execution; binding time changes the engineering problem.

### Problem it addressed

Postponing selection retains flexibility but may also retain code, configuration hazards and transition obligations.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Postponing selection retains flexibility but may also retain code, configuration hazards and transition obligations.

### Mechanism

Choose a binding time for each variation by weighing when information becomes available against footprint, assurance, deployment and runtime transition costs.

### Trigger or context

A variation point is introduced or its resolution time changes.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Bind early when the required choice is already known and later change has no justified value.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Timing of product knowledge, resource limits, supported changes and operational safety constraints.

### Domain profile

## ESPLE-014 — Feature-to-realisation traceability

Historical source pointer: `/properties/13` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Domain features and implementation variation points are related through mappings, which may be many-to-many and cross artefact boundaries.

### Problem it addressed

A selected feature has no realisation, or a shared implementation change affects products that nobody can identify.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A selected feature has no realisation, or a shared implementation change affects products that nobody can identify.

### Mechanism

Preserve the feature-to-artefact relations needed for derivation, change impact and diagnosis, including build selection, generator input and shared implementation.

### Trigger or context

Consequential changes or product construction depend on understanding how a feature is realised.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Local naming, module structure and a small checked mapping can replace a comprehensive trace database.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Feature semantics, actual artefacts and the consumers of impact or derivation information.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Realisation choices serve the selected family scope rather than requiring all products to share one implementation technique.",

  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Selection-to-implementation relations must preserve feature meaning; syntactic modularity alone does not establish semantic alignment.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A selected feature has no realisation, or a shared implementation change affects products that nobody can identify. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Developers, product builders and assurance engineers; a trace owner maintains evidence but does not infer semantic equivalence from a link alone.

### Actor and authority boundary

Developers, product builders and assurance engineers; a trace owner maintains evidence but does not infer semantic equivalence from a link alone. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Feature semantics, actual artefacts and the consumers of impact or derivation information.; the result must expose A relevant selection or change can be followed to affected realisation and products, with known unmapped areas exposed. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A relevant selection or change can be followed to affected realisation and products, with known unmapped areas exposed.

## ESPLE-015 — A portfolio of variability realisation techniques

Historical source pointer: `/properties/14` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Annotation, composition, components, parameters and transformation-based techniques realise variability at different granularities.",

    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",

    "ESPLE-S017": "2009",

    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Annotation, composition, components, parameters and transformation-based techniques realise variability at different granularities.

### Problem it addressed

A single preferred mechanism is imposed on variations with incompatible granularity, binding or interaction needs.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A single preferred mechanism is imposed on variations with incompatible granularity, binding or interaction needs.

### Mechanism

Select among conditional code, parameters, component/plugin substitution, inheritance, aspects, feature modules and transformations according to local constraints; explicitly manage cross-mechanism interactions.

### Trigger or context

Designing or restructuring the realisation of family variation.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Retain an ordinary parameter or small conditional when it is understandable and adequately checked.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Required binding time, feature granularity, language/tool constraints and change patterns.

### Domain profile

## ESPLE-016 — Ordered and conditional delta composition

Historical source pointer: `/properties/15` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Delta-oriented programming derives variants by conditionally applying ordered modifications to a base.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S018"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S018": "2010"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Delta-oriented programming derives variants by conditionally applying ordered modifications to a base.

### Problem it addressed

Purely additive feature composition struggles with variants requiring removal or replacement of existing behaviour.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Purely additive feature composition struggles with variants requiring removal or replacement of existing behaviour.

### Mechanism

Define applicable deltas, their dependencies or order and the validity conditions of each modification; verify the resulting product rather than assuming arbitrary edits commute.

### Trigger or context

Product differences naturally require structured additions, removals or changes over a shared base.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Use direct modules or parameters where no meaningful transformation sequence is needed.

### Dependencies or preconditions

A base program, delta applicability conditions, ordering constraints and language-specific well-formedness checks.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Realisation choices serve the selected family scope rather than requiring all products to share one implementation technique.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Shared code, product-specific code and interaction glue may coexist; arbitrary duplication is neither automatically valuable nor forbidden.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Selection-to-implementation relations must preserve feature meaning; syntactic modularity alone does not establish semantic alignment.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile, deployment and runtime binding carry different footprint, timing and transition obligations; later is not universally better.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Assets may be annotated, composed, parameterised or transformed; the core need not be one monolithic source tree.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Generator, template, build and dependency versions can affect the realised variant and belong in relevant provenance.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Cross-mechanism interactions require checks; successful code generation or compilation is not product correctness.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changes to realisation or binding time can invalidate mappings, supported configurations and previous assurance.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Implementers choose within family obligations; product-local exceptions require explicit support and do not inherit all shared claims.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Technique complexity must be earned by demand and assurance needs; ordinary parameters, modules or manual derivation remain alternatives.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P4",

    "FAMILY": "P4",
    "TRIGGER": "Product differences naturally require structured additions, removals or changes over a shared base.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "A base program, delta applicability conditions, ordering constraints and language-specific well-formedness checks.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Define applicable deltas, their dependencies or order and the validity conditions of each modification; verify the resulting product rather than assuming arbitrary edits commute.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Each selected delta is applicable in its context and the ordered derivation yields a well-formed product meeting selected requirements.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Use direct modules or parameters where no meaningful transformation sequence is needed.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Replace deltas with a simpler representation when the transformation structure no longer provides useful reuse or separation."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Purely additive feature composition struggles with variants requiring removal or replacement of existing behaviour. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product-line implementers and type/consistency analysers; a delta is a proposed construction step, not unrestricted authority to modify any supported product.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product-line implementers and type/consistency analysers; a delta is a proposed construction step, not unrestricted authority to modify any supported product. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: A base program, delta applicability conditions, ordering constraints and language-specific well-formedness checks.; the result must expose Each selected delta is applicable in its context and the ordered derivation yields a well-formed product meeting selected requirements. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Each selected delta is applicable in its context and the ordered derivation yields a well-formed product meeting selected requirements.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Order conflicts and base assumptions can be subtle; mechanism-level examples do not establish lower industrial lifecycle cost.",
  "Additional representation, applicability checks and maintenance of transformations as the base evolves."
]
```
