## ESPLE-017 — Validation of generator realisation

Historical source pointer: `/properties/16` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Generative approaches turn product specifications into artefacts; generation itself can introduce defects or omit intended features.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S006",
    "ESPLE-S016",
    "ESPLE-S021"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S006": "2000",
    "ESPLE-S016": "2004",
    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Generative approaches turn product specifications into artefacts; generation itself can introduce defects or omit intended features.

### Problem it addressed

A valid configuration is translated incorrectly by a generator, template, transformation or build process.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A valid configuration is translated incorrectly by a generator, template, transformation or build process.

### Mechanism

Validate the mapping from selections and asset versions to generated structure and behaviour, using applicable checks on transformations and produced products.

### Trigger or context

Product derivation relies on generation or nontrivial transformation of shared assets.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Review a simple deterministic template and test representative outputs; do not require a universal proof of the generator.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Intended realisation relation, generator/version identity and an oracle independent of merely successful generation.

### Domain profile

### Decision or consumer

Generator maintainers and product acceptance engineers exchange failing configurations and output evidence.

### Actor and authority boundary

Generator maintainers and product acceptance engineers exchange failing configurations and output evidence. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

Selected and excluded capabilities have the intended realised effects; failures are attributable to the configuration and derivation baseline.

## ESPLE-018 — Reusable non-code assets with consumers

Historical source pointer: `/properties/17` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{

    "ESPLE-S033": "2021-04"

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Core assets include requirements, architecture, tests, documentation, tools and production knowledge, not only reusable code.

### Problem it addressed

Code is shared but every product must rediscover its requirements, test basis or construction instructions.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Code is shared but every product must rediscover its requirements, test basis or construction instructions.

### Mechanism

Treat non-code work products as reusable assets where products actually consume them, with compatible versioning and variation information.

### Trigger or context

Repeated product work depends on shared engineering knowledge beyond implementation code.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Share only the few requirements, tests or instructions that have a real consumer; a full document suite is not obligatory.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Identified product consumers, scope of reuse and responsibility for keeping the asset valid.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Core assets and production plans are bounded by the supported family and its product obligations.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common assets include consumed non-code knowledge; variability is intentional in both artefacts and production decisions.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "A product configuration has meaning only with feature semantics and compatible realisation inputs.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Production records identify where consequential choices are resolved; runtime variability remains an additional product obligation.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Requirements, architecture, code, tests, documentation and tools are core assets only where products actually use them.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product identity joins configuration, relevant asset/tool versions and realised outputs; required repeatability must name its equivalence level.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Derivation provenance supports diagnosis and evidence transfer but does not establish feature-interaction correctness.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Old products may depend on older tools and assets; current-mainline availability is not a substitute for support continuity.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Code is shared but every product must rediscover its requirements, test basis or construction instructions. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Requirements, development, assurance and product-production participants according to the artefact; public templates do not establish use.

### Actor and authority boundary

Requirements, development, assurance and product-production participants according to the artefact; public templates do not establish use. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Identified product consumers, scope of reuse and responsibility for keeping the asset valid.; the result must expose A product can identify which shared non-code asset supports its construction or acceptance and which parts are product-specific. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A product can identify which shared non-code asset supports its construction or acceptance and which parts are product-specific.

### Known failure modes

```json
]
```

## ESPLE-019 — Family architectural invariants and variation boundaries

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Family design protects common structures while making intentional variation possible; a reference architecture can be guidance or an actual production constraint.

### Problem it addressed

Derived products violate assumptions on which reusable assets and family-level reasoning depend.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Derived products violate assumptions on which reusable assets and family-level reasoning depend.

### Mechanism

State the architectural invariants required for reuse and the permitted substitutions; enforce or check them in the derivation path where the claim requires enforcement.

### Trigger or context

Shared components, interfaces or assurance depend on product architecture remaining within a known class.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A few stable interfaces and reviewed component choices can suffice; an elaborate reference architecture is unnecessary for a small family.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Common obligations, variation points and evidence about what the real production process constrains.

### Domain profile

### Actor and authority boundary

Family architects and product implementers; a reference diagram alone cannot prohibit an actual implementation choice. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Common obligations, variation points and evidence about what the real production process constrains.; the result must expose A derived product either satisfies the relevant invariants or carries an explicit exception that limits inherited guarantees. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

## ESPLE-020 — A versioned product-derivation plan

Historical source pointer: `/properties/19` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "A production plan explains how core assets are used to build products, including roles, inputs, sequence and tool compatibility.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S011"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

A production plan explains how core assets are used to build products, including roles, inputs, sequence and tool compatibility.

### Problem it addressed

The family repository exists, but product production depends on undocumented personal knowledge.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

The family repository exists, but product production depends on undocumented personal knowledge.

### Mechanism

Maintain an actionable, version-related derivation procedure that tells builders which decisions, assets, tools and checks produce a supported product.

### Trigger or context

Products must be reproduced, handed to another team or built repeatedly from shared assets.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A short script plus explicit human decisions can be a complete plan for a small stable family.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Asset catalogue, configuration decisions, compatible tool versions and product acceptance criteria.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Core assets and production plans are bounded by the supported family and its product obligations.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common assets include consumed non-code knowledge; variability is intentional in both artefacts and production decisions.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "A product configuration has meaning only with feature semantics and compatible realisation inputs.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Production records identify where consequential choices are resolved; runtime variability remains an additional product obligation.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Requirements, architecture, code, tests, documentation and tools are core assets only where products actually use them.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product identity joins configuration, relevant asset/tool versions and realised outputs; required repeatability must name its equivalence level.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Derivation provenance supports diagnosis and evidence transfer but does not establish feature-interaction correctness.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Old products may depend on older tools and assets; current-mainline availability is not a substitute for support continuity.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Builders execute production plans; asset maintainers own changes and product authorities accept results or exceptions.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Automation and provenance detail are proportionate to volume, risk and support; a short checked manual procedure can suffice.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P5",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P5",
    "TRIGGER": "Products must be reproduced, handed to another team or built repeatedly from shared assets.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Asset catalogue, configuration decisions, compatible tool versions and product acceptance criteria.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Maintain an actionable, version-related derivation procedure that tells builders which decisions, assets, tools and checks produce a supported product.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A competent builder can derive the intended product from declared inputs and identify unsupported deviations.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A short script plus explicit human decisions can be a complete plan for a small stable family.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Delete unused steps and retire plans for unsupported baselines; retain what is needed to reproduce obligations still in force."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: The family repository exists, but product production depends on undocumented personal knowledge. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product builders execute the plan and return obstacles to maintainers; the plan does not itself accept a release.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product builders execute the plan and return obstacles to maintainers; the plan does not itself accept a release. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Asset catalogue, configuration decisions, compatible tool versions and product acceptance criteria.; the result must expose A competent builder can derive the intended product from declared inputs and identify unsupported deviations. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A competent builder can derive the intended product from declared inputs and identify unsupported deviations.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "A plan can lag the actual process or prescribe steps with no useful output. Full automation may be costly without sufficient volume.",
  "Keeping instructions and executable production infrastructure consistent."
]
```

Mature response: Maintain an actionable, version-related derivation procedure that tells builders which decisions, assets, tools and checks produce a supported product.",

```json
      "JUDGEMENT": "Retain production control, not maximal documentation or universal byte-for-byte build identity. This is a synthesis of distinct evidence, not a reported comparative trial of provenance policies."
    }
  ],

}
```

## ESPLE-021 — Configuration, asset and tool baseline identity

Historical source pointer: `/properties/20` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Production planning links products to versions of assets, tools and configuration decisions.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S021",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",

    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Production planning links products to versions of assets, tools and configuration decisions.

### Problem it addressed

A defect or result cannot be associated with the exact variant and baseline that produced it.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A defect or result cannot be associated with the exact variant and baseline that produced it.

### Mechanism

Identify the configuration and the relevant asset, generator, dependency and tool versions together; distinguish requested configuration from realised product identity.

### Trigger or context

Reproduction, support, change impact or transfer of an assurance result depends on knowing what was built.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A versioned configuration file and pinned build inputs may suffice; a new provenance service is not inherently required.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Actual build inputs and the equivalence level required for support or assurance.

### Domain profile

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A defect or result cannot be associated with the exact variant and baseline that produced it. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Builders, maintainers and acceptance reviewers exchange product identities; an identifier is evidence of naming, not proof of semantic correctness.

### Actor and authority boundary

Builders, maintainers and acceptance reviewers exchange product identities; an identifier is evidence of naming, not proof of semantic correctness. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Actual build inputs and the equivalence level required for support or assurance.; the result must expose A reported product can be traced to its actual derivation inputs and distinguished from a similarly named but different baseline. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A reported product can be traced to its actual derivation inputs and distinguished from a similarly named but different baseline.

## ESPLE-022 — Repeatable derivation at a declared equivalence level

Historical source pointer: `/properties/21` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Defined derivation makes repeatable product construction possible; exact binary reproducibility is a stronger, separate condition.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S016",
    "ESPLE-S021"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S016": "2004",
    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Defined derivation makes repeatable product construction possible; exact binary reproducibility is a stronger, separate condition.

### Problem it addressed

Repeating the apparent same derivation silently produces a materially different product.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Repeating the apparent same derivation silently produces a materially different product.

### Mechanism

Declare whether repeatability means equal feature realisation, equivalent tested behaviour, identical generated source or identical bytes, then control the inputs necessary for that claim.

### Trigger or context

Support, certification, comparison or distribution requires reliable reconstruction of a product.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Behavioural or structural equivalence may be adequate; do not impose bit-for-bit output where timestamps or tool differences are immaterial.

### Dependencies or preconditions

A declared equivalence criterion, relevant environment inputs and known nondeterministic steps.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Core assets and production plans are bounded by the supported family and its product obligations.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common assets include consumed non-code knowledge; variability is intentional in both artefacts and production decisions.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "A product configuration has meaning only with feature semantics and compatible realisation inputs.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Production records identify where consequential choices are resolved; runtime variability remains an additional product obligation.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Requirements, architecture, code, tests, documentation and tools are core assets only where products actually use them.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product identity joins configuration, relevant asset/tool versions and realised outputs; required repeatability must name its equivalence level.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Derivation provenance supports diagnosis and evidence transfer but does not establish feature-interaction correctness.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Old products may depend on older tools and assets; current-mainline availability is not a substitute for support continuity.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Builders execute production plans; asset maintainers own changes and product authorities accept results or exceptions.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Automation and provenance detail are proportionate to volume, risk and support; a short checked manual procedure can suffice.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P5",

    "FAMILY": "P5",
    "TRIGGER": "Support, certification, comparison or distribution requires reliable reconstruction of a product.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "A declared equivalence criterion, relevant environment inputs and known nondeterministic steps.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Declare whether repeatability means equal feature realisation, equivalent tested behaviour, identical generated source or identical bytes, then control the inputs necessary for that claim.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A repeat derivation meets the declared equivalence criterion, or differences are explained and adjudicated.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Behavioural or structural equivalence may be adequate; do not impose bit-for-bit output where timestamps or tool differences are immaterial.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Relax controls whose stronger identity is no longer consumed, while retaining enough information for supported products."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Repeating the apparent same derivation silently produces a materially different product. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Builders and assurance consumers agree the needed repeatability; the strongest possible level is not automatically economically justified.

### Actor and authority boundary

Builders and assurance consumers agree the needed repeatability; the strongest possible level is not automatically economically justified. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: A declared equivalence criterion, relevant environment inputs and known nondeterministic steps.; the result must expose A repeat derivation meets the declared equivalence criterion, or differences are explained and adjudicated. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A repeat derivation meets the declared equivalence criterion, or differences are explained and adjudicated.

## ESPLE-023 — Layer-specific assurance claims

Historical source pointer: `/properties/22` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Product-line analyses establish different kinds of result over models, code, configurations and behaviour.",

    "ESPLE-S031"

    "ESPLE-S031": "2023"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Product-line analyses establish different kinds of result over models, code, configurations and behaviour.

### Problem it addressed

Evidence at one layer is promoted into a stronger claim about the entire product family.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Evidence at one layer is promoted into a stronger claim about the entire product family.

### Mechanism

State each assurance claim's object, quantified configurations, inputs, environment and baseline; identify the realisation obligations needed to carry it to a product.

### Trigger or context

A model, test, proof or analysis result is used to justify a product or family decision.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A concise claim-and-boundary statement attached to the result can be sufficient.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Actual analysis semantics, configuration coverage, tool limitations and product identity.

### Domain profile

```json
{
```

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Evidence at one layer is promoted into a stronger claim about the entire product family. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Actor and authority boundary

Assurance reviewers and release decision-makers consume bounded claims; analysts do not acquire release authority by producing evidence. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

It is possible to tell what the result establishes, what it leaves open and which derived products may inherit it.

## ESPLE-024 — Interaction-aware coverage

Historical source pointer: `/properties/23` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Feature interactions can create behaviour that no isolated feature analysis reveals.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S017",
    "ESPLE-S019",
    "ESPLE-S020",
    "ESPLE-S021"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S017": "2009",

    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Feature interactions can create behaviour that no isolated feature analysis reveals.

### Problem it addressed

Products fail only when particular combinations, inputs or environments occur together.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Products fail only when particular combinations, inputs or environments occur together.

### Mechanism

Identify plausible interaction mechanisms and select checks whose coverage matches their order and risk; include shared-asset and environmental interactions, not only adjacent feature-tree nodes.

### Trigger or context

Features share state, resources, protocols, build choices or behavioural assumptions.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Isolated independent substitutions may need only interface checks; do not impose high-order combinatorial testing without an interaction rationale.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Dependency evidence, known faults, changed features and the operational conditions under which they combine.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Assurance quantifies over a declared configuration set, not an undefined claim about every possible product.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing analysis requires common semantics and represented variability; shared defects and omitted assumptions can affect many variants.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Model validity, implementation well-formedness and behavioural correctness are distinct claims and need explicit connecting premises.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile-time selection, build layers and runtime transitions change the space that an assurance method must cover.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Reusable tests, contracts and conditional results can support several products only while their assumptions remain true.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Evidence must identify the model, code, tool and product baseline to which it applies.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Coverage includes configurations, interactions, inputs and environments; pairwise feature coverage is not complete behavioural coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changed constraints or shared assets can invalidate samples, contracts, test oracles and inherited results.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Analysts provide evidence; product acceptance and risk decisions remain with authorised consumers, including product-specific exceptions.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Choose product, feature, family or hybrid checking by claim, scale and cost; exhaustive analysis may be cheaper for a small supported set.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P6",

    "FAMILY": "P6",
    "TRIGGER": "Features share state, resources, protocols, build choices or behavioural assumptions.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Dependency evidence, known faults, changed features and the operational conditions under which they combine.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Identify plausible interaction mechanisms and select checks whose coverage matches their order and risk; include shared-asset and environmental interactions, not only adjacent feature-tree nodes.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Important interaction hypotheses have tests, analysis or an explicit unresolved disposition; coverage statements identify what was not exercised.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Isolated independent substitutions may need only interface checks; do not impose high-order combinatorial testing without an interaction rationale.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove checks only when the interaction is structurally impossible, no longer supported or covered by a demonstrably adequate alternative."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Products fail only when particular combinations, inputs or environments occur together. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Feature developers and assurance engineers exchange interaction hypotheses and failures; product owners decide acceptable residual risk.

### Actor and authority boundary

Feature developers and assurance engineers exchange interaction hypotheses and failures; product owners decide acceptable residual risk. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Dependency evidence, known faults, changed features and the operational conditions under which they combine.; the result must expose Important interaction hypotheses have tests, analysis or an explicit unresolved disposition; coverage statements identify what was not exercised. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Important interaction hypotheses have tests, analysis or an explicit unresolved disposition; coverage statements identify what was not exercised.

```json
]
```

## ESPLE-025 — Constrained and risk-informed sampling

Historical source pointer: `/properties/24` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Sampling reduces the number of configured products analysed, including combinatorial and coverage-oriented strategies.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S019",
    "ESPLE-S020",
    "ESPLE-S021"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S019": "2014",
    "ESPLE-S020": "2016",
    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Sampling reduces the number of configured products analysed, including combinatorial and coverage-oriented strategies.

### Problem it addressed

Exhaustive product testing is too expensive, but an arbitrary sample leaves its omissions unexplained.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Exhaustive product testing is too expensive, but an arbitrary sample leaves its omissions unexplained.

### Mechanism

Sample valid configurations using a declared objective and budget, include critical known combinations, and report residual higher-order or environmental coverage limits.

### Trigger or context

Full enumeration is infeasible and a bounded sample can provide useful assurance.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Test every supported configuration when the family is small, or analyse a relevant shared property once when a sound family analysis is cheaper.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Configuration constraints, test cost, fault-risk assumptions and a meaningful comparison baseline.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Assurance quantifies over a declared configuration set, not an undefined claim about every possible product.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing analysis requires common semantics and represented variability; shared defects and omitted assumptions can affect many variants.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Model validity, implementation well-formedness and behavioural correctness are distinct claims and need explicit connecting premises.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile-time selection, build layers and runtime transitions change the space that an assurance method must cover.",

  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Evidence must identify the model, code, tool and product baseline to which it applies.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Coverage includes configurations, interactions, inputs and environments; pairwise feature coverage is not complete behavioural coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changed constraints or shared assets can invalidate samples, contracts, test oracles and inherited results.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Analysts provide evidence; product acceptance and risk decisions remain with authorised consumers, including product-specific exceptions.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Choose product, feature, family or hybrid checking by claim, scale and cost; exhaustive analysis may be cheaper for a small supported set.",

    "FAMILY": "P6",

    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Test every supported configuration when the family is small, or analyse a relevant shared property once when a sound family analysis is cheaper.",

```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Exhaustive product testing is too expensive, but an arbitrary sample leaves its omissions unexplained. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Test planners and assurance consumers choose the coverage trade-off; a sampler cannot declare all unsampled products correct.

### Actor and authority boundary

Test planners and assurance consumers choose the coverage trade-off; a sampler cannot declare all unsampled products correct. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Configuration constraints, test cost, fault-risk assumptions and a meaningful comparison baseline.; the result must expose The selected configurations are valid, the stated coverage is computed correctly and important omissions are visible. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The selected configurations are valid, the stated coverage is computed correctly and important omissions are visible.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Algorithm rankings change with constraints, headers, build systems and budgets; pairwise coverage cannot generally detect higher-order faults.",
  "Sampling computation, real build/test costs and maintaining risk assumptions."
]
```

```json
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_OBJECTION": "Algorithm rankings change with constraints, headers, build systems and budgets; pairwise coverage cannot generally detect higher-order faults.",
```

## ESPLE-026 — Family-based sharing of analysis

Historical source pointer: `/properties/25` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Family-based analyses share computation over variability rather than enumerate every product separately.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S019",
    "ESPLE-S022",
    "ESPLE-S040"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S019": "2014",
    "ESPLE-S022": "2011",
    "ESPLE-S040": "2018"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Family-based analyses share computation over variability rather than enumerate every product separately.

### Problem it addressed

Repeated analysis duplicates work over common code and cannot scale with the number of configurations.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Repeated analysis duplicates work over common code and cannot scale with the number of configurations.

### Mechanism

Preserve variation symbolically or structurally during analysis so common computations are reused, while retaining conditions under which each result applies.

### Trigger or context

Many products share analyzable structure and the analysis implementation supports their variability semantics.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Ordinary product analysis is preferable for a few configurations or unsupported languages and artefacts.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Correct variability-aware representation, configuration constraints and a sound underlying analysis for the claim in question.

### Domain profile

```json
{

  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing analysis requires common semantics and represented variability; shared defects and omitted assumptions can affect many variants.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Model validity, implementation well-formedness and behavioural correctness are distinct claims and need explicit connecting premises.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile-time selection, build layers and runtime transitions change the space that an assurance method must cover.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Reusable tests, contracts and conditional results can support several products only while their assumptions remain true.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Evidence must identify the model, code, tool and product baseline to which it applies.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Coverage includes configurations, interactions, inputs and environments; pairwise feature coverage is not complete behavioural coverage.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changed constraints or shared assets can invalidate samples, contracts, test oracles and inherited results.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Analysts provide evidence; product acceptance and risk decisions remain with authorised consumers, including product-specific exceptions.",

    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Results correspond to the intended per-product analysis across the represented configurations, with conditional warnings and unsupported cases exposed.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Ordinary product analysis is preferable for a few configurations or unsupported languages and artefacts.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Repeated analysis duplicates work over common code and cannot scale with the number of configurations. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Analysis engineers produce conditional results for developers and reviewers; coverage of configurations is not coverage of every kind of bug.

### Actor and authority boundary

Analysis engineers produce conditional results for developers and reviewers; coverage of configurations is not coverage of every kind of bug. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Correct variability-aware representation, configuration constraints and a sound underlying analysis for the claim in question.; the result must expose Results correspond to the intended per-product analysis across the represented configurations, with conditional warnings and unsupported cases exposed. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Results correspond to the intended per-product analysis across the represented configurations, with conditional warnings and unsupported cases exposed.

### Known failure modes

```json

  "State explosion can remain; preprocessor/build omissions undermine coverage; reported warnings may be false positives rather than confirmed defects.",
  "Specialised infrastructure, memory, model extraction and interpretation of conditional results."
]
```

## ESPLE-027 — Compositional feature contracts

Historical source pointer: `/properties/26` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Feature-wise and compositional reasoning attempts to combine local results under explicit interfaces and assumptions.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S016",
    "ESPLE-S017",
    "ESPLE-S019"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S016": "2004",
    "ESPLE-S017": "2009",
    "ESPLE-S019": "2014"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Feature-wise and compositional reasoning attempts to combine local results under explicit interfaces and assumptions.

### Problem it addressed

Rechecking every product is costly, but isolated feature correctness may not survive composition.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Rechecking every product is costly, but isolated feature correctness may not survive composition.

### Mechanism

State the assumptions and guarantees of a feature or component and discharge compatibility and interaction obligations when composing selected parts.

### Trigger or context

Stable interfaces and separable properties make local reasoning reusable across products.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Check the whole derived product when contracts would be harder to specify or maintain than the verification itself.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Explicit environmental assumptions, composition semantics and evidence that the actual interfaces meet the contracts.

### Domain profile

```json
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P6",
    "TRIGGER": "Stable interfaces and separable properties make local reasoning reusable across products.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Explicit environmental assumptions, composition semantics and evidence that the actual interfaces meet the contracts.",

    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Abandon a compositional claim when its interface or independence assumptions fail; use product-level checking instead."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Rechecking every product is costly, but isolated feature correctness may not survive composition. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Feature developers and integration/assurance engineers jointly discharge assumptions; no module can certify unexamined partners.

### Actor and authority boundary

Feature developers and integration/assurance engineers jointly discharge assumptions; no module can certify unexamined partners. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Explicit environmental assumptions, composition semantics and evidence that the actual interfaces meet the contracts.; the result must expose A product-level claim follows from the local results and checked composition premises, not merely from all modules passing tests individually. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A product-level claim follows from the local results and checked composition premises, not merely from all modules passing tests individually.

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Application engineering tests particular products in addition to reusable domain assets.

### Problem it addressed

A product inherits a family label despite a defective build, omitted requirement or product-specific integration problem.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A product inherits a family label despite a defective build, omitted requirement or product-specific integration problem.

### Mechanism

Check the actual derived artefacts against the selected product obligations and operating context, reusing family evidence only where its premises apply.

### Trigger or context

A product is delivered, deployed or materially changed.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Reuse valid existing evidence and perform only the additional product checks needed for unestablished obligations; repeated full testing is not automatically required.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Product identity, selected requirements, environment and applicable family-level results.

## ESPLE-029 — Analysis of build, macro and configuration layers

Historical source pointer: `/properties/28` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Variability can be encoded in macros, headers, build selection, configuration management and non-code generation inputs.",
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Variability can be encoded in macros, headers, build selection, configuration management and non-code generation inputs.

### Problem it addressed

Analysis of a source file or feature model omits another layer that changes which code exists in a product.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Analysis of a source file or feature model omits another layer that changes which code exists in a product.

### Mechanism

Include the configuration layers relevant to the claimed result and track approximation or unsupported constructs in the extraction chain.

### Trigger or context

Build, dependency or generator configuration affects product realisation or static-analysis reach.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A single checked configuration layer is enough when the derivation genuinely has no hidden variation elsewhere.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Build rules, macro definitions, selected versions and non-code artefacts that influence inclusion or generated interfaces.

### Domain profile

```json
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Analysis of a source file or feature model omits another layer that changes which code exists in a product. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Build and analysis engineers reconcile the representation; a parser warning or missing extractor needs an explicit assurance disposition.

### Actor and authority boundary

Build and analysis engineers reconcile the representation; a parser warning or missing extractor needs an explicit assurance disposition. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Build rules, macro definitions, selected versions and non-code artefacts that influence inclusion or generated interfaces.; the result must expose Conditional results refer to the actual relevant product space, or clearly state which layers were excluded. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Conditional results refer to the actual relevant product space, or clearly state which layers were excluded.

## ESPLE-030 — Semantic classification of feature-model edits

Historical source pointer: `/properties/29` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Feature-model edit analysis distinguishes changes by their effect on represented configuration sets.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S023",
    "ESPLE-S024",
    "ESPLE-S039"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S023": "2009",
    "ESPLE-S024": "2013 preprint/online; 2014 journal",
    "ESPLE-S039": "2025"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Feature-model edit analysis distinguishes changes by their effect on represented configuration sets.

### Problem it addressed

Syntactically small edits silently add or remove permitted products.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Syntactically small edits silently add or remove permitted products.

### Mechanism

Compare old and new model semantics using an explicit feature correspondence; classify preserved, added and removed configurations before selecting migration or regression work.

### Trigger or context

A feature model, cross-tree constraint or feature vocabulary changes.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Enumerate and compare all configurations for a small family rather than introduce a specialised solver.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Both model versions, feature identity mapping and the intended semantic relation.

### Domain profile

```json
{
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Syntactically small edits silently add or remove permitted products. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Modellers, product maintainers and test planners consume the classification; set preservation does not certify unchanged implementation behaviour.

### Actor and authority boundary

Modellers, product maintainers and test planners consume the classification; set preservation does not certify unchanged implementation behaviour. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Both model versions, feature identity mapping and the intended semantic relation.; the result must expose The semantic effect is supported by witnesses, counterexamples or a justified equivalence result under the stated mapping. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The semantic effect is supported by witnesses, counterexamples or a justified equivalence result under the stated mapping.

## ESPLE-031 — Intent-preserving configuration migration

Historical source pointer: `/properties/30` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Configuration migration can require sequences of changes, feature-model drift and preservation of supported product obligations.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S024",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S024": "2013 preprint/online; 2014 journal",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Configuration migration can require sequences of changes, feature-model drift and preservation of supported product obligations.

### Problem it addressed

An old configuration becomes invalid, or a valid replacement no longer fulfils the product's intended role.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

An old configuration becomes invalid, or a valid replacement no longer fulfils the product's intended role.

### Mechanism

Map old selections and product intent to a permitted new configuration, accounting for intermediate constraints, change costs and the actual resulting product.

### Trigger or context

A supported product must move to a changed model, core baseline or production environment.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Retain a supported older baseline or use a reviewed manual migration when the portfolio is small.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Old identity, new constraints, stakeholder intent, supported transition paths and acceptance evidence.

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: An old configuration becomes invalid, or a valid replacement no longer fulfils the product's intended role. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product maintainers and stakeholders approve changed selections; a migration optimiser proposes solutions within authorised objectives.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product maintainers and stakeholders approve changed selections; a migration optimiser proposes solutions within authorised objectives. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Old identity, new constraints, stakeholder intent, supported transition paths and acceptance evidence.; the result must expose The migrated product satisfies the relevant new constraints and preserved obligations, or explicit accepted changes are recorded. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The migrated product satisfies the relevant new constraints and preserved obligations, or explicit accepted changes are recorded.

## ESPLE-032 — Impact and regression analysis of shared changes

Historical source pointer: `/properties/31` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

A change to a shared asset can affect multiple products and their configurations.

### Problem it addressed

A beneficial shared fix introduces regressions or breaks an obligation in a supported variant.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A beneficial shared fix introduces regressions or breaks an obligation in a supported variant.

### Mechanism

Identify affected products and assumptions, select appropriate regression evidence and coordinate propagation, exceptions or supported parallel versions.

### Trigger or context

A shared requirement, component, test, model or production tool changes materially.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Use local regression when the changed asset is demonstrably product-local; reuse established evidence for unaffected products.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Traceable product baselines, change semantics, dependencies and supported obligations.

### Domain profile

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A beneficial shared fix introduces regressions or breaks an obligation in a supported variant. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Shared-asset and product maintainers communicate impact; a core maintainer cannot erase a product obligation merely by merging a change.

### Actor and authority boundary

Shared-asset and product maintainers communicate impact; a core maintainer cannot erase a product obligation merely by merging a change. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Traceable product baselines, change semantics, dependencies and supported obligations.; the result must expose Affected supported variants have an impact disposition, and propagated fixes are bound to the tested or justified product versions. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Affected supported variants have an impact disposition, and propagated fixes are bound to the tested or justified product versions.

## ESPLE-033 — Supported baseline and release relationships

Historical source pointer: `/properties/32` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Production plans and industrial families relate product releases to supported core-asset and tool versions.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S024",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S024": "2013 preprint/online; 2014 journal",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Production plans and industrial families relate product releases to supported core-asset and tool versions.

### Problem it addressed

Independently released products silently consume incompatible assets or lose maintenance support when the core moves ahead.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Independently released products silently consume incompatible assets or lose maintenance support when the core moves ahead.

### Mechanism

State compatible baseline combinations and supported release relationships; allow coordinated or independent product schedules while making upgrade and support obligations explicit.

### Trigger or context

Products, core assets or tools evolve on different schedules.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A single synchronised release may be adequate when all products and stakeholders can genuinely move together.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Compatibility evidence, support commitments and product delivery schedules.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Evolution preserves or explicitly revises obligations of supported products, not just the newest family model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Some variation remains valuable only for legacy support; recovery from products must distinguish history from desired future scope.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature names and model syntax can change without semantic change, and stable names can conceal changed intent.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Migration can change binding or traverse intermediate configurations; live transitions require the separate dynamic model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Changes may affect code, tests, requirements, generators, interfaces and production plans together.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Old and new product identities and compatibility relations must remain distinguishable during migration or parallel support.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Regression work targets affected variants and assumptions; model-set preservation alone does not establish behaviour preservation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Migration, deprecation, test repair and retirement have different consumers and triggers; support-aware retirement need not retain new-product admission.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Core and product maintainers coordinate fixes; a shared change cannot silently override an exceptional product obligation.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Retaining multiple baselines has costs, but premature consolidation can break products; cloning or deferred migration can be the adequate alternative.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P7",

    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "State compatible baseline combinations and supported release relationships; allow coordinated or independent product schedules while making upgrade and support obligations explicit.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Each supported product has an identifiable maintenance path and compatible production baseline.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A single synchronised release may be adequate when all products and stakeholders can genuinely move together.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Retire a baseline through an explicit support decision and migration or end-of-life path, not by deleting its name from the current model."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Independently released products silently consume incompatible assets or lose maintenance support when the core moves ahead. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Release and product maintainers agree the supported combinations; the newest core version is not automatically authoritative for all products.

### Actor and authority boundary

Release and product maintainers agree the supported combinations; the newest core version is not automatically authoritative for all products. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Compatibility evidence, support commitments and product delivery schedules.; the result must expose Each supported product has an identifiable maintenance path and compatible production baseline. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Each supported product has an identifiable maintenance path and compatible production baseline.

```json
]
```

## ESPLE-034 — Recovery of variability with domain reconciliation

Historical source pointer: `/properties/33` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Extractive adoption and reverse engineering recover variation from existing products and implementation artefacts.",

    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",

    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"

}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Extractive adoption and reverse engineering recover variation from existing products and implementation artefacts.

### Problem it addressed

A useful family exists operationally, but its variation model is missing, stale or inconsistent with implementation.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A useful family exists operationally, but its variation model is missing, stale or inconsistent with implementation.

### Mechanism

Recover candidate features and constraints from multiple artefact types, preserve existing valid domain knowledge and review recovered relations against product intent.

### Trigger or context

Consolidating variants, repairing stale models or reducing dependence on tacit configuration knowledge.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Compare a few existing products manually; do not introduce a general extraction framework for a tiny stable portfolio.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Relevant versions, configurations, artefact semantics and expert knowledge of what the code does not express.

### Domain profile

## ESPLE-035 — Co-evolution of configuration-oriented tests

Historical source pointer: `/properties/34` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Evolving feature models can invalidate configuration-oriented test suites; repair, regeneration and change-specific selection are distinct strategies.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S037"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S037": "2025 online; 2026 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Evolving feature models can invalidate configuration-oriented test suites; repair, regeneration and change-specific selection are distinct strategies.

### Problem it addressed

Old tests exercise invalid or irrelevant configurations while newly introduced configurations receive no attention.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Old tests exercise invalid or irrelevant configurations while newly introduced configurations receive no attention.

### Mechanism

Reassess test-configuration validity and purpose after model change; choose repair, fresh generation or targeted new-product tests according to regression and novelty objectives.

### Trigger or context

A feature-model change alters permitted products or test-selection assumptions.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Retain still-valid tests and manually add a few relevant configurations when changes are small.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Old tests, old/new model semantics, test costs and the risk of old versus newly admitted configurations.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Evolution preserves or explicitly revises obligations of supported products, not just the newest family model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Some variation remains valuable only for legacy support; recovery from products must distinguish history from desired future scope.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature names and model syntax can change without semantic change, and stable names can conceal changed intent.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Migration can change binding or traverse intermediate configurations; live transitions require the separate dynamic model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Changes may affect code, tests, requirements, generators, interfaces and production plans together.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Old and new product identities and compatibility relations must remain distinguishable during migration or parallel support.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Regression work targets affected variants and assumptions; model-set preservation alone does not establish behaviour preservation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Migration, deprecation, test repair and retirement have different consumers and triggers; support-aware retirement need not retain new-product admission.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Core and product maintainers coordinate fixes; a shared change cannot silently override an exceptional product obligation.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Retaining multiple baselines has costs, but premature consolidation can break products; cloning or deferred migration can be the adequate alternative.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P7",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P7",
    "TRIGGER": "A feature-model change alters permitted products or test-selection assumptions.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Old tests, old/new model semantics, test costs and the risk of old versus newly admitted configurations.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Reassess test-configuration validity and purpose after model change; choose repair, fresh generation or targeted new-product tests according to regression and novelty objectives.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "The suite's selected configurations remain valid and its coverage purpose addresses the relevant change; behavioural oracles are separately checked.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Retain still-valid tests and manually add a few relevant configurations when changes are small.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove tests for unsupported configurations only after support decisions; retain a cheap fresh-generation path when repairing costs more."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Old tests exercise invalid or irrelevant configurations while newly introduced configurations receive no attention. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Test engineers and product assurance consumers choose the objective; similarity of configurations is not automatically low behavioural test-maintenance effort.

### Actor and authority boundary

Test engineers and product assurance consumers choose the objective; similarity of configurations is not automatically low behavioural test-maintenance effort. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Old tests, old/new model semantics, test costs and the risk of old versus newly admitted configurations.; the result must expose The suite's selected configurations remain valid and its coverage purpose addresses the relevant change; behavioural oracles are separately checked. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The suite's selected configurations remain valid and its coverage purpose addresses the relevant change; behavioural oracles are separately checked.
