## ESPLE-036 — Lifecycle economics with uncertainty

Historical source pointer: `/properties/35` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Product-line economic models compare organisational investment, reusable assets and product construction with alternatives.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S012"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S012": "2005-02"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Product-line economic models compare organisational investment, reusable assets and product construction with alternatives.

### Problem it addressed

Reuse savings are celebrated while startup, coordination, assurance, migration and exit costs are omitted.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Reuse savings are celebrated while startup, coordination, assurance, migration and exit costs are omitted.

### Mechanism

Compare lifecycle costs and required outcomes over an explicit horizon; vary demand, reuse rate and maintenance assumptions rather than rely on a universal break-even count.

### Trigger or context

An investment or continuation decision depends on product-line benefits.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Use a coarse scenario comparison when precise estimates are not credible; keep assumptions visible rather than manufacture precision.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Alternative costs, expected products, asset investment, ongoing coordination and support commitments.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Family scope and expected product demand are assumptions in the economic case, not benefits created by writing a model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Reuse frequency and stable commonality determine whether shared investment can pay; uncertainty must remain visible.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature-space size can estimate effort but is not equivalent to customer value or realised product demand.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Binding choices shift cost between preparation, builds, deployment and runtime operation.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Asset investment includes requirements, implementation, production tools, assurance and ongoing coordination, not just reusable code.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Comparable alternatives need equivalent product and support outcomes; configuration identity supports measuring actual work rather than counting labels.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Assurance cost and consequences of shared defects belong in the comparison; cheaper development is not adequate if mandatory outcomes fail.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Maintenance, migration, deprecation and exit costs matter over the selected horizon; sunk costs do not settle prospective decisions.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Shared benefits and costs fall on different teams; incentives and product autonomy affect whether nominal savings materialise.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Independent development, selective reuse, modular single products and clone-and-own are legitimate comparators; no universal break-even count is retained.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P8",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P8",
    "TRIGGER": "An investment or continuation decision depends on product-line benefits.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Alternative costs, expected products, asset investment, ongoing coordination and support commitments.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Compare lifecycle costs and required outcomes over an explicit horizon; vary demand, reuse rate and maintenance assumptions rather than rely on a universal break-even count.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "The decision states the horizon, counterfactual, omitted costs and sensitivity that could reverse the preferred option.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Use a coarse scenario comparison when precise estimates are not credible; keep assumptions visible rather than manufacture precision.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Replace estimates with actual evidence as available; stop modelling details too small to change the decision."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Reuse savings are celebrated while startup, coordination, assurance, migration and exit costs are omitted. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Portfolio sponsors and engineering managers review estimates with those bearing the costs; an economic model is not a realised-return measurement.

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio sponsors and engineering managers review estimates with those bearing the costs; an economic model is not a realised-return measurement. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Alternative costs, expected products, asset investment, ongoing coordination and support commitments.; the result must expose The decision states the horizon, counterfactual, omitted costs and sensitivity that could reverse the preferred option. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The decision states the horizon, counterfactual, omitted costs and sensitivity that could reverse the preferred option.

## ESPLE-037 — An explicit adequate non-product-line alternative

### Original form

Industrial cloning evidence and incremental adoption challenge the idea that planned reuse is the only respectable option.

### Problem it addressed

A product line is selected without comparison to an adequate simpler method.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A product line is selected without comparison to an adequate simpler method.

### Mechanism

Compare SPLE with clone-and-own, selective component reuse, a modular single product or independent development under the same quality and support obligations.

### Trigger or context

Choosing or defending shared family production.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep the adequate non-product-line method when its total burden is lower or the product-line benefit is not established.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Actual portfolio size and variation, ownership autonomy, defect risk and comparable outcome requirements.

```json
{
```

## ESPLE-038 — Economic reassessment without a sunk-cost veto

Historical source pointer: `/properties/37` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Candidate origin

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Product-focused scope and lifecycle economic reasoning require ongoing justification, not only a founding business case.

### Problem it addressed

Past investment becomes the reason to retain a costly family structure even after demand or assumptions change.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Past investment becomes the reason to retain a costly family structure even after demand or assumptions change.

### Mechanism

Reassess future costs and obligations at material changes; narrow, split, retire or continue the family based on prospective adequacy, preserving outstanding support.

### Trigger or context

Demand changes, reuse disappoints, coordination dominates or the product portfolio contracts.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A brief decision review using actual product experience may replace a recurrent formal business-case ceremony.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Current support obligations, observed costs and credible future alternatives.

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio sponsors with product and asset maintainers decide future investment; sunk costs explain history but cannot by themselves justify continuation. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Current support obligations, observed costs and credible future alternatives.; the result must expose Continued shared production has a current rationale, or a bounded transition away from it preserves necessary commitments. ### Observable postcondition

## ESPLE-039 — Authority and arbitration for shared assets

Historical source pointer: `/properties/38` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Product-line management addresses ownership and conflicting needs around shared assets and production.

### Problem it addressed

Teams can identify a shared problem but nobody can authorise a fix, or one product imposes its priorities on all others.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Teams can identify a shared problem but nobody can authorise a fix, or one product imposes its priorities on all others.

### Mechanism

Make contribution, variation and release decision rights explicit; provide a proportionate way to adjudicate competing product obligations and fund accepted shared work.

### Trigger or context

Shared changes affect products with different priorities or authorities.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A named maintainer and direct product-owner agreement can suffice; a new committee is not a property.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Ownership, affected obligations, cost bearers and an escalation path for unresolved conflict.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Organisational reach and participant autonomy bound the family; an ecosystem may not be one controlled product line.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing can concern code, specifications or interfaces; those forms create different control and compatibility relationships.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features can encode customer or external constraints, but their legal/security applicability must come from competent external evidence.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Authority to bind or rebind a choice is separate from its representability in a model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Core assets have maintainers and consumers; externally owned components and autonomous derivatives are not silently internal assets.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product and asset provenance supports accountability without granting ownership over external participants.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "A common core can create common-mode exposure; external compatibility and product-specific obligations remain part of assurance.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Independent release schedules, suppliers and support commitments constrain shared changes and retirement.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Contribution, variation, exception and release authority must be explicit and proportionate; a committee is not universally required.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination, training and bottlenecks are costs; simpler ownership or ordinary dependency management can be adequate where no family governance is needed.",

    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Make contribution, variation and release decision rights explicit; provide a proportionate way to adjudicate competing product obligations and fund accepted shared work.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A disputed shared change can receive an accountable decision, and affected products learn its consequences.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "A named maintainer and direct product-owner agreement can suffice; a new committee is not a property.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Simplify authority arrangements when the portfolio or conflict surface shrinks; preserve accountability for supported products."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Teams can identify a shared problem but nobody can authorise a fix, or one product imposes its priorities on all others. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product and core decision-makers exchange impact and priority information; evidence of a technical opportunity is separate from authority to act.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product and core decision-makers exchange impact and priority information; evidence of a technical opportunity is separate from authority to act. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Ownership, affected obligations, cost bearers and an escalation path for unresolved conflict.; the result must expose A disputed shared change can receive an accountable decision, and affected products learn its consequences. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A disputed shared change can receive an accountable decision, and affected products learn its consequences.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Central ownership can become a queue and incentive bottleneck. Open ecosystems may lack a single authority and require negotiated boundaries instead.",
  "Negotiation, delay and the cost of shared priorities that do not maximise every product locally."
]
```

## ESPLE-040 — Capability, incentives and resource alignment

Historical source pointer: `/properties/39` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Organisational readiness and management are part of product-line adoption rather than consequences of installing tools.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [

  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S008": "2001 publication; commonly cited 2002",
    "ESPLE-S012": "2005-02",
    "ESPLE-S013": "2010"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Organisational readiness and management are part of product-line adoption rather than consequences of installing tools.

### Problem it addressed

Teams are rewarded for immediate local delivery while expected to invest in shared assets and absorb others' requirements.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Teams are rewarded for immediate local delivery while expected to invest in shared assets and absorb others' requirements.

### Mechanism

Align skills, capacity, incentives and cost allocation with the intended shared-production responsibilities; scale commitments to actual capability.

### Trigger or context

A product-line plan requires work or coordination beyond the existing organisation's supported practice.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Start with a limited shared capability and existing staff rather than create a full platform organisation before demand is known.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Skill gaps, delivery commitments, maintenance capacity and how shared costs and benefits are distributed.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Organisational reach and participant autonomy bound the family; an ecosystem may not be one controlled product line.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing can concern code, specifications or interfaces; those forms create different control and compatibility relationships.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features can encode customer or external constraints, but their legal/security applicability must come from competent external evidence.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Authority to bind or rebind a choice is separate from its representability in a model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Core assets have maintainers and consumers; externally owned components and autonomous derivatives are not silently internal assets.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product and asset provenance supports accountability without granting ownership over external participants.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "A common core can create common-mode exposure; external compatibility and product-specific obligations remain part of assurance.",

  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P9",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P9",

    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Accepted shared work has capacity and ownership, and product teams can obtain needed support without hidden unpaid obligations.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Start with a limited shared capability and existing staff rather than create a full platform organisation before demand is known.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Teams are rewarded for immediate local delivery while expected to invest in shared assets and absorb others' requirements. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Engineering managers, product teams and sponsors negotiate resourcing; training completion alone does not establish operational capability.

### Actor and authority boundary

Engineering managers, product teams and sponsors negotiate resourcing; training completion alone does not establish operational capability. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Skill gaps, delivery commitments, maintenance capacity and how shared costs and benefits are distributed.; the result must expose Accepted shared work has capacity and ownership, and product teams can obtain needed support without hidden unpaid obligations. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Accepted shared work has capacity and ownership, and product teams can obtain needed support without hidden unpaid obligations.

### Known failure modes

```json
[
  "Reorganisation can cost more than reuse saves; no universal team structure follows from the tradition.",
  "Training, staffing, changed incentives and transition disruption."
]
```

### Important criticisms

## ESPLE-041 — An explicit ecosystem participation boundary

Historical source pointer: `/properties/40` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Ecosystem work expands variability concerns beyond one organisation; community-driven variability may coordinate specifications without a shared implementation core.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S029",
    "ESPLE-S044"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S029": "2009",
    "ESPLE-S044": "2026-03-21"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

DOCUMENTED_EXTERNAL_RELATION

### Original form

Ecosystem work expands variability concerns beyond one organisation; community-driven variability may coordinate specifications without a shared implementation core.

### Problem it addressed

A family model assumes control over external contributors or treats every interoperating derivative as a centrally derived product.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A family model assumes control over external contributors or treats every interoperating derivative as a centrally derived product.

### Mechanism

Identify who controls assets, proposals, interfaces and releases; distinguish contractual or negotiated compatibility from internal derivation authority; export bounded interfaces rather than fictitious central control.

### Trigger or context

Suppliers, open-source participants or independent platform users determine material variation.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Manage a bounded component dependency when no broader ecosystem coordination is required.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Participant autonomy, shared interface/specification obligations and actual contribution or compatibility mechanisms.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Organisational reach and participant autonomy bound the family; an ecosystem may not be one controlled product line.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing can concern code, specifications or interfaces; those forms create different control and compatibility relationships.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Features can encode customer or external constraints, but their legal/security applicability must come from competent external evidence.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Authority to bind or rebind a choice is separate from its representability in a model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Core assets have maintainers and consumers; externally owned components and autonomous derivatives are not silently internal assets.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product and asset provenance supports accountability without granting ownership over external participants.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "A common core can create common-mode exposure; external compatibility and product-specific obligations remain part of assurance.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Independent release schedules, suppliers and support commitments constrain shared changes and retirement.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Contribution, variation, exception and release authority must be explicit and proportionate; a committee is not universally required.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination, training and bottlenecks are costs; simpler ownership or ordinary dependency management can be adequate where no family governance is needed.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P9",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P9",
    "TRIGGER": "Suppliers, open-source participants or independent platform users determine material variation.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Participant autonomy, shared interface/specification obligations and actual contribution or compatibility mechanisms.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Identify who controls assets, proposals, interfaces and releases; distinguish contractual or negotiated compatibility from internal derivation authority; export bounded interfaces rather than fictitious central control.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Shared claims identify their organisational reach; independent products are not silently counted as conforming family instances.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Manage a bounded component dependency when no broader ecosystem coordination is required.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Omit ecosystem machinery when the relevant relationship is only ordinary dependency consumption."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A family model assumes control over external contributors or treats every interoperating derivative as a centrally derived product. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Platform maintainers and external participants negotiate evidence and compatibility; one organisation cannot unilaterally authorise changes to autonomous derivatives.

### Actor and authority boundary

Platform maintainers and external participants negotiate evidence and compatibility; one organisation cannot unilaterally authorise changes to autonomous derivatives. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Participant autonomy, shared interface/specification obligations and actual contribution or compatibility mechanisms.; the result must expose Shared claims identify their organisational reach; independent products are not silently counted as conforming family instances. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Shared claims identify their organisational reach; independent products are not silently counted as conforming family instances.

### Known failure modes

```json

  "Ecosystem taxonomy does not prove effective governance or economic superiority. Central product-line controls can be both infeasible and inappropriate in autonomous communities.",
  "Negotiation, compatibility testing and incomplete knowledge of independently evolving implementations."
]
```

## ESPLE-042 — Traceability of externally specified obligations

Historical source pointer: `/properties/41` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Domain requirements and product variation can be driven by market legislation, hardware, customers and externally specified constraints.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S009",
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S033",
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S009": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S033": "2021-04",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

DOCUMENTED_EXTERNAL_RELATION

### Original form

Domain requirements and product variation can be driven by market legislation, hardware, customers and externally specified constraints.

### Problem it addressed

Sharing an asset is mistaken for sharing all permissions, security obligations or support conditions across products.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Sharing an asset is mistaken for sharing all permissions, security obligations or support conditions across products.

### Mechanism

Associate externally supplied obligations with affected assets and products, then require appropriate evidence or specialist adjudication before deriving or changing those products.

### Trigger or context

Supplier conditions, security requirements, licences, regulation or support commitments constrain valid product use.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Preserve the relevant external requirement and its affected products; do not invent a new compliance framework or a legal conclusion.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Authoritative external requirements, applicable versions and an identified competent decision-maker.

### Domain profile

```json
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Associate externally supplied obligations with affected assets and products, then require appropriate evidence or specialist adjudication before deriving or changing those products.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A shared change cannot silently erase product-specific obligations; unresolved applicability is visible to the authorised consumer.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Preserve the relevant external requirement and its affected products; do not invent a new compliance framework or a legal conclusion.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove a constraint only when its external basis or affected supported products genuinely cease to apply."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Sharing an asset is mistaken for sharing all permissions, security obligations or support conditions across products. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product owners, relevant specialists and asset maintainers exchange obligations and impact evidence; SPLE analysis does not grant legal permission or certify security.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product owners, relevant specialists and asset maintainers exchange obligations and impact evidence; SPLE analysis does not grant legal permission or certify security. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Authoritative external requirements, applicable versions and an identified competent decision-maker.; the result must expose A shared change cannot silently erase product-specific obligations; unresolved applicability is visible to the authorised consumer. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A shared change cannot silently erase product-specific obligations; unresolved applicability is visible to the authorised consumer.

## ESPLE-043 — Context-to-configuration mapping for dynamic families

Historical source pointer: `/properties/42` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Dynamic product lines combine runtime context observation with constrained selection and reconfiguration of features.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S030",
    "ESPLE-S031",
    "ESPLE-S043"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S030": "2008",
    "ESPLE-S031": "2023",
    "ESPLE-S043": "2008"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Dynamic product lines combine runtime context observation with constrained selection and reconfiguration of features.

### Problem it addressed

Runtime adaptation is improvised without a known family of admissible alternatives or a relation between context and capability.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Runtime adaptation is improvised without a known family of admissible alternatives or a relation between context and capability.

### Mechanism

Model permitted runtime configurations, relevant context and the mapping from observations to proposed reconfiguration; connect the decision to actual actuators and feedback.

### Trigger or context

A product must change its selected capabilities during execution as conditions change.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Select a static or deployment-time variant when runtime rebinding is not required.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Observable context, supported configurations, adaptation goals and a feasible reconfiguration mechanism.

### Domain profile


### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Runtime adaptation is improvised without a known family of admissible alternatives or a relation between context and capability. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Operators and adaptation designers define the allowed policy; a runtime controller acts only within the authority and capabilities supplied to it.

### Actor and authority boundary

Operators and adaptation designers define the allowed policy; a runtime controller acts only within the authority and capabilities supplied to it. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Observable context, supported configurations, adaptation goals and a feasible reconfiguration mechanism.; the result must expose A context change leads to a permitted, realised configuration or a diagnosed inability to reconfigure; actual state is observed afterwards. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A context change leads to a permitted, realised configuration or a diagnosed inability to reconfigure; actual state is observed afterwards.

## ESPLE-044 — Transition safety and state continuity

Historical source pointer: `/properties/43` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Dynamic variability introduces transition requirements in addition to endpoint configuration validity.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S030",
    "ESPLE-S031"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S030": "2008",
    "ESPLE-S031": "2023"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Dynamic variability introduces transition requirements in addition to endpoint configuration validity.

### Problem it addressed

Two individually acceptable configurations can be connected only through an unsafe intermediate state or loss of operational state.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Two individually acceptable configurations can be connected only through an unsafe intermediate state or loss of operational state.

### Mechanism

Check the transition path, quiescence or state transfer and actual controllability; preserve safety requirements throughout the operating phase or enter a separately justified safe reconfiguration phase.

### Trigger or context

Features or components are rebound while the system is operating.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Stop and restart in a known safe condition, or prohibit runtime switching, when live transition assurance is disproportionate.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Current state, intermediate-state constraints, controllable actions, timing assumptions and recovery behaviour.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Dynamic and AI-assisted variants remain bounded by a declared family; broader ecosystem derivatives may require a different organisational model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "A permitted set of alternatives is not permission to change arbitrarily; learned suggestions do not establish new valid commonality.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Static configuration constraints, runtime requirements and learned proposal validity must be distinguished.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Runtime rebinding introduces transition paths, state continuity and actual controllability; fixed features do not imply environment-independent execution.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Models, controllers, generators and validation assets participate in derivation, but each has a separate realisation and maintenance burden.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Observed runtime state and generated output need identity distinct from the requested configuration or prompt.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Safe endpoints do not establish safe transitions; proofs require adequate plant models and generated outputs require independent validation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model and tool changes can invalidate runtime policies or regenerated variants; continuity must be checked rather than presumed.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Operators and accountable product authorities authorise actions; formal models and learned assistants possess no self-created operational authority.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Offline selection, deterministic transformations or no adaptation may dominate runtime/AI machinery when their extra capability has no justified consumer.",

    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Check the transition path, quiescence or state transfer and actual controllability; preserve safety requirements throughout the operating phase or enter a separately justified safe reconfiguration phase.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "The realised transition respects the declared operational invariants and state-continuity obligations, not only its final feature selection.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Stop and restart in a known safe condition, or prohibit runtime switching, when live transition assurance is disproportionate.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove live-transition machinery if the product is constrained to safe offline reconfiguration."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Two individually acceptable configurations can be connected only through an unsafe intermediate state or loss of operational state. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Adaptation and safety/operations decision-makers approve the transition envelope; endpoint SAT checks are insufficient.

### Actor and authority boundary

Adaptation and safety/operations decision-makers approve the transition envelope; endpoint SAT checks are insufficient. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Current state, intermediate-state constraints, controllable actions, timing assumptions and recovery behaviour.; the result must expose The realised transition respects the declared operational invariants and state-continuity obligations, not only its final feature selection. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The realised transition respects the declared operational invariants and state-continuity obligations, not only its final feature selection.

## ESPLE-045 — Supervisory control under plant assumptions

Historical source pointer: `/properties/44` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Supervisory control is imported into dynamic feature configuration using discrete-event models and controllable/uncontrollable events.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S031"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S031": "2023"
  }

```

### Lineage class

DOCUMENTED_IMPORT

### Original form

Supervisory control is imported into dynamic feature configuration using discrete-event models and controllable/uncontrollable events.

### Problem it addressed

A runtime controller may allow requirement-violating behaviour or block necessary completion when features change.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A runtime controller may allow requirement-violating behaviour or block necessary completion when features change.

### Mechanism

Synthesize a supervisor for the modelled plant and requirements, then establish that observations, event controllability and actuation match the implementation.

### Trigger or context

A finite-state or otherwise supported discrete-event model adequately captures a consequential dynamic product-line problem.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A reviewed transition table or offline configuration can suffice where synthesis adds no justified assurance.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Plant model, marked states, requirements, event partition and a faithful implementation mapping.

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A runtime controller may allow requirement-violating behaviour or block necessary completion when features change. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Control and product engineers assess model adequacy and deploy within operational authority; a theorem does not grant new actuator powers.

### Actor and authority boundary

Control and product engineers assess model adequacy and deploy within operational authority; a theorem does not grant new actuator powers. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Plant model, marked states, requirements, event partition and a faithful implementation mapping.; the result must expose Within the model, the supervisor is safe, controllable and nonblocking as specified; implementation checks support transfer of the relevant result. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Within the model, the supervisor is safe, controllable and nonblocking as specified; implementation checks support transfer of the relevant result.

## ESPLE-046 — Semantic fidelity in variability interchange

Historical source pointer: `/properties/45` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "UVL addresses feature-model interchange through defined language levels; standards and tools have distinct scopes.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S032",
    "ESPLE-S033"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S032": "2025",
    "ESPLE-S033": "2021-04"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

UVL addresses feature-model interchange through defined language levels; standards and tools have distinct scopes.

### Problem it addressed

A model parses in another tool but loses constraints, value semantics or configuration meaning.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A model parses in another tool but loses constraints, value semantics or configuration meaning.

### Mechanism

Specify the supported language level and check semantic preservation for translations and toolchains; make unsupported constructs explicit.

### Trigger or context

Variability models move across tools, organisations or analysis backends.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep one stable representation and tool when interchange has no real consumer.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Source and destination semantics, supported versions and a meaningful equivalence or conformance test.

### Domain profile

```json
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Models, controllers, generators and validation assets participate in derivation, but each has a separate realisation and maintenance burden.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Observed runtime state and generated output need identity distinct from the requested configuration or prompt.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Safe endpoints do not establish safe transitions; proofs require adequate plant models and generated outputs require independent validation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model and tool changes can invalidate runtime policies or regenerated variants; continuity must be checked rather than presumed.",

  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A model parses in another tool but loses constraints, value semantics or configuration meaning. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Tool integrators and model consumers agree the interchange contract; a shared extension does not automatically become a normative standard.

### Actor and authority boundary

Tool integrators and model consumers agree the interchange contract; a shared extension does not automatically become a normative standard. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Source and destination semantics, supported versions and a meaningful equivalence or conformance test.; the result must expose Relevant configurations and constraints survive interchange, or the loss is detected before relying on the result. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Relevant configurations and constraints survive interchange, or the loss is detected before relying on the result.

### Lineage class

HYBRIDISATION

### Original form

Recent LLM work proposes generation or co-evolution assistance; adjacent DSL studies examine migration quality and scaling limits.

### Problem it addressed

A plausible generated configuration, migration or implementation is mistaken for a validated product change.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A plausible generated configuration, migration or implementation is mistaken for a validated product change.

### Mechanism

Treat generated changes as proposals; validate syntax, constraints, semantic intent, realisation and product obligations using appropriate independent checks before acceptance.

### Trigger or context

Learned tools assist feature modelling, configuration advice, migration or variant generation.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Use deterministic constraint solving, a conventional transformation or manual review when these are cheaper and adequate.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Versioned input/output, preserved intent, an acceptance oracle and authority independent of the proposal generator.

## ESPLE-048 — LLM regeneration as a superior replacement for maintained variability

Historical source pointer: `/properties/47` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "A June 2026 exploratory proposal suggests producing variants by LLM regeneration from specifications rather than maintaining conventional variability structures.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S034"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S034": "2026-06-17"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

HYBRIDISATION

### Original form

A June 2026 exploratory proposal suggests producing variants by LLM regeneration from specifications rather than maintaining conventional variability structures.

### Problem it addressed

Conventional variability can be costly to maintain, but regeneration may lose provenance, consistency and accumulated assurance.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Conventional variability can be costly to maintain, but regeneration may lose provenance, consistency and accumulated assurance.

### Mechanism

The candidate is a replacement strategy: retain specifications and regenerate variants. Its stronger claim of superior lifecycle adequacy is not established by the inspected evidence.

### Trigger or context

Considering regeneration as a substitute for a maintained family realisation, not merely as assistance.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep maintained assets and use an assistant only for bounded changes; conventional generation remains an alternative.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Stable specifications, independently checked outputs, versioned models/tooling and longitudinal cost/defect evidence.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Dynamic and AI-assisted variants remain bounded by a declared family; broader ecosystem derivatives may require a different organisational model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "A permitted set of alternatives is not permission to change arbitrarily; learned suggestions do not establish new valid commonality.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Static configuration constraints, runtime requirements and learned proposal validity must be distinguished.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Runtime rebinding introduces transition paths, state continuity and actual controllability; fixed features do not imply environment-independent execution.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Models, controllers, generators and validation assets participate in derivation, but each has a separate realisation and maintenance burden.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Observed runtime state and generated output need identity distinct from the requested configuration or prompt.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Safe endpoints do not establish safe transitions; proofs require adequate plant models and generated outputs require independent validation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Model and tool changes can invalidate runtime policies or regenerated variants; continuity must be checked rather than presumed.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Operators and accountable product authorities authorise actions; formal models and learned assistants possess no self-created operational authority.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Offline selection, deterministic transformations or no adaptation may dominate runtime/AI machinery when their extra capability has no justified consumer.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P10",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P10",
    "TRIGGER": "Considering regeneration as a substitute for a maintained family realisation, not merely as assistance.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Stable specifications, independently checked outputs, versioned models/tooling and longitudinal cost/defect evidence.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "The candidate is a replacement strategy: retain specifications and regenerate variants. Its stronger claim of superior lifecycle adequacy is not established by the inspected evidence.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "A discriminating evaluation would preserve obligations across repeated variants and changes while comparing full costs and defects against a competent maintained-family baseline.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Keep maintained assets and use an assistant only for bounded changes; conventional generation remains an alternative.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Reject a trial replacement when output continuity or lifecycle cost cannot be established; retain useful bounded assistance separately."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Conventional variability can be costly to maintain, but regeneration may lose provenance, consistency and accumulated assurance. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product and assurance owners would have to accept the replacement; the study does not authorise such adoption.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product and assurance owners would have to accept the replacement; the study does not authorise such adoption. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Stable specifications, independently checked outputs, versioned models/tooling and longitudinal cost/defect evidence.; the result must expose A discriminating evaluation would preserve obligations across repeated variants and changes while comparing full costs and defects against a competent maintained-family baseline. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

A discriminating evaluation would preserve obligations across repeated variants and changes while comparing full costs and defects against a competent maintained-family baseline.

### Original form

Adversarial universalisation of illustrative break-even claims; not attributed to all product-line researchers.

### Problem it addressed

A fixed product count is substituted for a context-specific investment comparison.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A fixed product count is substituted for a context-specific investment comparison.

### Mechanism

No general mechanism supports the universal threshold. Replace the claim with a horizon-specific comparison of investment, derivation, coordination and support costs.

### Trigger or context

A proposed adoption is justified by reaching a supposedly universal number of products.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A small scenario calculation showing the relevant cost drivers is adequate to reject the fixed threshold.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Actual cost structure, demand uncertainty and a credible non-product-line baseline.

## ESPLE-050 — A complete upfront platform as a prerequisite

Historical source pointer: `/properties/49` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Adversarial reading of proactive domain engineering as a compulsory complete upfront platform.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S010"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S010": "2001 workshop; 2002 proceedings"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial reading of proactive domain engineering as a compulsory complete upfront platform.

### Problem it addressed

Adoption is blocked or over-invested because every possible variation is demanded before any product experience.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Adoption is blocked or over-invested because every possible variation is demanded before any product experience.

### Mechanism

Reject the universal prerequisite; use the proactive, reactive, extractive or hybrid route justified by the actual starting conditions.

### Trigger or context

A process requires complete domain assets before permitting useful product work.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Start with a bounded common asset and one product, then expand only when evidence warrants it.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Current assets, product urgency and uncertainty about future variation.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Domain and application work are justified by a family whose reusable capabilities serve actual or credible products.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "The common core and product-local work are related but not identical; repeated local differences require examination, not automatic generalisation.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Product requests can expose absent or mistaken features and constraints; feedback may change the model or preserve an exception.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Shared and local decisions can bind at different stages; adoption strategy must not predetermine all technical binding choices.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Domain work maintains reusable assets; application work derives products and returns feasibility and failure evidence.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Product observations need enough baseline and configuration identity to be actionable across the family.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Local success does not prove safe generalisation; shared repairs need product-impact analysis.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Feedback is recurrent and can interrupt planned development; extractive, reactive and proactive routes can coexist over time.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "The same people may hold both responsibilities; exceptions retain support accountability and limits on inherited guarantees.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Coordination and asset preparation are real costs; a small shared capability or continued cloning can dominate full platform adoption.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P2",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P2",
    "TRIGGER": "A process requires complete domain assets before permitting useful product work.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Current assets, product urgency and uncertainty about future variation.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Reject the universal prerequisite; use the proactive, reactive, extractive or hybrid route justified by the actual starting conditions.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Upfront work is limited to what the chosen route and product obligations actually require.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Start with a bounded common asset and one product, then expand only when evidence warrants it.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove the compulsory rule; retain bounded proactive investment where it earns its cost."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Adoption is blocked or over-invested because every possible variation is demanded before any product experience. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Portfolio and engineering leads choose a transition route; no method diagram establishes that all assets must precede all products.

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio and engineering leads choose a transition route; no method diagram establishes that all assets must precede all products. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Current assets, product urgency and uncertainty about future variation.; the result must expose Upfront work is limited to what the chosen route and product obligations actually require. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Upfront work is limited to what the chosen route and product obligations actually require.

## ESPLE-051 — A feature-tree artefact as sufficient variability management

Historical source pointer: `/properties/50` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Feature-tree-only observance is a caricature: original FODA included more than a diagram.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S005"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S005": "1990-11"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Feature-tree-only observance is a caricature: original FODA included more than a diagram.

### Problem it addressed

The presence of a model artefact is treated as proof of managed variability.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

The presence of a model artefact is treated as proof of managed variability.

### Mechanism

Reject artefact sufficiency; require meaningful features, usable constraints, a relation to realisation and an actual configuration or change consumer.

### Trigger or context

A diagram, modelling tool or documentation checklist is offered as the evidence of control.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A checked decision table or direct configuration rules may supply the required function without a tree.

### Dependencies or preconditions

The decision the artefact supports and evidence that it remains aligned with products.

### Domain profile

```json
{

  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Common and variable characteristics are explicit semantic decisions; historical product observations do not exhaust valid possibilities.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Boolean, cardinality, attribute and typed constraints describe encoded validity; domain adequacy must be checked outside solver consistency.",

  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Features relate to requirements and assets through possibly many-to-many mappings; neither a node nor a flag is automatically a module.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "A configuration identifies model selections under a particular model version; it is not yet the identity of a built product.",
```

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: The presence of a model artefact is treated as proof of managed variability. No numerical benefit is asserted.

Modellers and configurators should demonstrate use; reviewers must not infer fulfilment from a file's existence.

### Actor and authority boundary

Modellers and configurators should demonstrate use; reviewers must not infer fulfilment from a file's existence. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: The decision the artefact supports and evidence that it remains aligned with products.; the result must expose The artefact changes or supports a valid decision, or is identified as nonessential documentation. ### Observable postcondition

The artefact changes or supports a valid decision, or is identified as nonessential documentation.

## ESPLE-052 — Feature-model satisfiability as delivered-product correctness

Historical source pointer: `/properties/51` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Adversarial promotion of model satisfiability into a guarantee about delivered implementation behaviour.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S014"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S014": "2005"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial promotion of model satisfiability into a guarantee about delivered implementation behaviour.

### Problem it addressed

Omitted constraints or a defective generator produce incorrect products from an encoded-valid configuration.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Omitted constraints or a defective generator produce incorrect products from an encoded-valid configuration.

### Mechanism

Reject the implication. Keep model consistency, realisation correctness and product behaviour as separate claims with explicit connecting premises.

### Trigger or context

A SAT result is used to assert product correctness or release readiness.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Show a concrete missing domain constraint or faulty realisation to expose the gap; retain the SAT check for its genuine purpose.

### Dependencies or preconditions

The actual encoded formula, generator/implementation relation and product obligations.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Assurance quantifies over a declared configuration set, not an undefined claim about every possible product.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Sharing analysis requires common semantics and represented variability; shared defects and omitted assumptions can affect many variants.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Model validity, implementation well-formedness and behavioural correctness are distinct claims and need explicit connecting premises.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile-time selection, build layers and runtime transitions change the space that an assurance method must cover.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Reusable tests, contracts and conditional results can support several products only while their assumptions remain true.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Evidence must identify the model, code, tool and product baseline to which it applies.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Omitted constraints or a defective generator produce incorrect products from an encoded-valid configuration. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Assurance and release consumers require the missing evidence; the solver supplies only the stated logical result.

### Actor and authority boundary

Assurance and release consumers require the missing evidence; the solver supplies only the stated logical result. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: The actual encoded formula, generator/implementation relation and product obligations.; the result must expose The final claim no longer exceeds the model and encoding that were checked. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The final claim no longer exceeds the model and encoding that were checked.

## ESPLE-053 — Pairwise coverage as family correctness

Historical source pointer: `/properties/52` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json

    "ESPLE-S021": "2018 online; 2019 journal"
  }

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial overclaim that pairwise feature coverage establishes correctness of the whole family.

### Problem it addressed

A fault requires three or more interacting selections, or a specific input/environment not covered by configuration sampling.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A fault requires three or more interacting selections, or a specific input/environment not covered by configuration sampling.

### Mechanism

Reject the guarantee; use pairwise testing only as a bounded selection strategy within an explicit assurance argument.

### Trigger or context

A pairwise coverage percentage is presented as complete family assurance.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A four-row three-feature counterexample demonstrates the logical gap without a large experiment.

### Dependencies or preconditions

The actual tested configurations, coverage definition and the fault hypotheses relevant to the product.

### Actor and authority boundary

## ESPLE-054 — Later binding as universally better

Historical source pointer: `/properties/53` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Adversarial ranking that treats later binding as always more mature because it preserves choice.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S030",
    "ESPLE-S042",
    "ESPLE-S043"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S030": "2008",
    "ESPLE-S042": "2005",
    "ESPLE-S043": "2008"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial ranking that treats later binding as always more mature because it preserves choice.

### Problem it addressed

Unneeded runtime variation introduces retained code, state and transition hazards.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Unneeded runtime variation introduces retained code, state and transition hazards.

### Mechanism

Reject a universal ranking and select binding time by the timing of needed knowledge and the costs of retained flexibility.

### Trigger or context

A compile/deployment-time choice is moved later without a concrete operational need.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Fix the choice earlier and rebuild or redeploy when rare changes occur.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Change frequency, latency requirements, footprint and transition obligations.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Realisation choices serve the selected family scope rather than requiring all products to share one implementation technique.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Shared code, product-specific code and interaction glue may coexist; arbitrary duplication is neither automatically valuable nor forbidden.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Selection-to-implementation relations must preserve feature meaning; syntactic modularity alone does not establish semantic alignment.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Compile, deployment and runtime binding carry different footprint, timing and transition obligations; later is not universally better.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Assets may be annotated, composed, parameterised or transformed; the core need not be one monolithic source tree.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Generator, template, build and dependency versions can affect the realised variant and belong in relevant provenance.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Cross-mechanism interactions require checks; successful code generation or compilation is not product correctness.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Changes to realisation or binding time can invalidate mappings, supported configurations and previous assurance.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Implementers choose within family obligations; product-local exceptions require explicit support and do not inherit all shared claims.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Technique complexity must be earned by demand and assurance needs; ordinary parameters, modules or manual derivation remain alternatives.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P4",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P4",
    "TRIGGER": "A compile/deployment-time choice is moved later without a concrete operational need.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Change frequency, latency requirements, footprint and transition obligations.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Reject a universal ranking and select binding time by the timing of needed knowledge and the costs of retained flexibility.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "Each late-bound choice has a justified consumer and an assurance path for its extra reachable states.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Fix the choice earlier and rebuild or redeploy when rare changes occur.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Remove the universal preference, not the late-binding branch itself."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Unneeded runtime variation introduces retained code, state and transition hazards. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Architects and operators assess the actual need for late choice; flexibility is not an end in itself.

### Actor and authority boundary

Architects and operators assess the actual need for late choice; flexibility is not an end in itself. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Change frequency, latency requirements, footprint and transition obligations.; the result must expose Each late-bound choice has a justified consumer and an assurance path for its extra reachable states. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Each late-bound choice has a justified consumer and an assurance path for its extra reachable states.

## ESPLE-055 — A universal one-feature/one-module correspondence

Historical source pointer: `/properties/54` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

