```json
    "ESPLE-S041"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S017": "2009",
    "ESPLE-S018": "2010",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial assumption that every stakeholder feature corresponds to exactly one implementation module and vice versa.

### Problem it addressed

Shared code, crosscutting concerns and generated artefacts are hidden to preserve an attractive diagram.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Shared code, crosscutting concerns and generated artefacts are hidden to preserve an attractive diagram.

### Mechanism

Reject the universal correspondence; model the relations needed for derivation and impact, allowing many-to-many mappings and interaction artefacts.

### Trigger or context

Traceability or architecture forces a one-to-one feature/module decomposition.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep a simple mapping where it genuinely exists; do not introduce complexity solely to avoid one-to-one cases.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Feature semantics and actual realisation structure rather than a desired visual symmetry.

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Shared code, crosscutting concerns and generated artefacts are hidden to preserve an attractive diagram. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Feature modellers and implementers reconcile their abstractions; neither representation automatically dictates the other.

### Actor and authority boundary

Feature modellers and implementers reconcile their abstractions; neither representation automatically dictates the other. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Feature semantics and actual realisation structure rather than a desired visual symmetry.; the result must expose Shared and crosscutting realisations remain visible and their consequences can be checked. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Shared and crosscutting realisations remain visible and their consequences can be checked.

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial conflation of specialised automated feature-based production with the whole SPLE tradition.

### Problem it addressed

A small well-managed family is rejected as not a product line because some derivation choices remain manual.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A small well-managed family is rejected as not a product line because some derivation choices remain manual.

### Mechanism

Reject total automation as a universal definition; require a defined, adequate derivation method and evidence for the resulting products.

### Trigger or context

Automation percentage is used as the criterion for product-line existence or maturity.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A reliable reviewed manual derivation can be sufficient for a small stable family.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Product volume, error consequences, required repeatability and a real production plan.

### Domain profile

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A small well-managed family is rejected as not a product line because some derivation choices remain manual. No numerical benefit is asserted.

Product builders and managers judge production adequacy; narrower standard or method definitions must be named rather than universalised.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product builders and managers judge production adequacy; narrower standard or method definitions must be named rather than universalised. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Product volume, error consequences, required repeatability and a real production plan.; the result must expose The family can reliably derive supported products, with consequential manual decisions explicit and checked. ### Observable postcondition

The family can reliably derive supported products, with consequential manual decisions explicit and checked.

## ESPLE-057 — Clone-and-own as universally inadmissible

Historical source pointer: `/properties/56` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
  "SOURCE_DATES": {

```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial moralisation of clone-and-own as always inferior to managed shared production.

### Problem it addressed

Product autonomy, startup speed or low expected reuse are ignored in selecting an engineering strategy.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Product autonomy, startup speed or low expected reuse are ignored in selecting an engineering strategy.

### Mechanism

Reject universal inadmissibility; compare cloning's immediate adequacy and future synchronisation costs with the actual product-line alternative.

### Trigger or context

Cloning is rejected without examining portfolio conditions or maintenance obligations.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Retain clones with sufficient provenance and targeted shared-fix tracking when they are the lower-cost adequate solution.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Product divergence, expected common changes, ownership constraints and a realistic lifecycle horizon.

### Domain profile

```json

  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature-space size can estimate effort but is not equivalent to customer value or realised product demand.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Binding choices shift cost between preparation, builds, deployment and runtime operation.",
```

## ESPLE-058 — Stable names or configuration sets as behavioural compatibility

Historical source pointer: `/properties/57` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Adversarial equivalence between stable feature names or permitted sets and preservation of product behaviour.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S023",
    "ESPLE-S024"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S023": "2009",
    "ESPLE-S024": "2013 preprint/online; 2014 journal"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial equivalence between stable feature names or permitted sets and preservation of product behaviour.

### Problem it addressed

The same selection yields changed behaviour after a core, generator or semantic update.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

The same selection yields changed behaviour after a core, generator or semantic update.

### Mechanism

Reject the equivalence; preserve or explicitly change feature intent and verify the actual migrated product's obligations.

### Trigger or context

Compatibility is asserted solely because names or model-level product sets did not change.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A targeted behavioural regression can be sufficient where a full migration analysis adds nothing.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Old/new feature meanings, realisation versions and relevant behavioural expectations.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Evolution preserves or explicitly revises obligations of supported products, not just the newest family model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Some variation remains valuable only for legacy support; recovery from products must distinguish history from desired future scope.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature names and model syntax can change without semantic change, and stable names can conceal changed intent.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Migration can change binding or traverse intermediate configurations; live transitions require the separate dynamic model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Changes may affect code, tests, requirements, generators, interfaces and production plans together.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Old and new product identities and compatibility relations must remain distinguishable during migration or parallel support.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Regression work targets affected variants and assumptions; model-set preservation alone does not establish behaviour preservation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Migration, deprecation, test repair and retirement have different consumers and triggers; support-aware retirement need not retain new-product admission.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Core and product maintainers coordinate fixes; a shared change cannot silently override an exceptional product obligation.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Retaining multiple baselines has costs, but premature consolidation can break products; cloning or deferred migration can be the adequate alternative.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: The same selection yields changed behaviour after a core, generator or semantic update. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Model and product maintainers communicate semantic change; model equivalence does not authorise silent behavioural changes.

### Actor and authority boundary

Model and product maintainers communicate semantic change; model equivalence does not authorise silent behavioural changes. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Old/new feature meanings, realisation versions and relevant behavioural expectations.; the result must expose Compatibility claims name what is preserved and are supported at that level. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Compatibility claims name what is preserved and are supported at that level.

```json
]
```

## ESPLE-059 — Raw configuration count as a benefit measure

Historical source pointer: `/properties/58` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Configuration counting is a legitimate analysis operation, but maximising that count is not an established engineering objective.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S012",
    "ESPLE-S015",
    "ESPLE-S045"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S012": "2005-02",
    "ESPLE-S015": "2010",
    "ESPLE-S045": "2026-04-17"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Configuration counting is a legitimate analysis operation, but maximising that count is not an established engineering objective.

### Problem it addressed

A larger theoretical product space is presented as benefit despite unused options and greater assurance burden.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

A larger theoretical product space is presented as benefit despite unused options and greater assurance burden.

### Mechanism

Use counts to estimate analysis, support or selection complexity; assess value through useful products and avoided costs rather than raw combinatorics.

### Trigger or context

Configuration-space size is used in an investment or maturity claim.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Count supported or demanded products and their costs when theoretical possibilities do not affect the decision.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Validity constraints, demand evidence and the purpose of the metric.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Family scope and expected product demand are assumptions in the economic case, not benefits created by writing a model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Reuse frequency and stable commonality determine whether shared investment can pay; uncertainty must remain visible.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature-space size can estimate effort but is not equivalent to customer value or realised product demand.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Binding choices shift cost between preparation, builds, deployment and runtime operation.",

  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Assurance cost and consequences of shared defects belong in the comparison; cheaper development is not adequate if mandatory outcomes fail.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Maintenance, migration, deprecation and exit costs matter over the selected horizon; sunk costs do not settle prospective decisions.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Shared benefits and costs fall on different teams; incentives and product autonomy affect whether nominal savings materialise.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Independent development, selective reuse, modular single products and clone-and-own are legitimate comparators; no universal break-even count is retained.",

    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Count supported or demanded products and their costs when theoretical possibilities do not affect the decision.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "Stop reporting the count when it has no decision consumer; retain it when needed to plan coverage or support."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: A larger theoretical product space is presented as benefit despite unused options and greater assurance burden. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

### Actor and authority boundary

Portfolio and assurance planners interpret the count; a metric collector cannot infer market value from arithmetic. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Validity constraints, demand evidence and the purpose of the metric.; the result must expose A reported count is tied to a concrete planning use and is not equated with realised benefit. ## ESPLE-060 — Formal modelling as operational authority

Historical source pointer: `/properties/59` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Analytical counter-proposition examined against the cited tradition; not attributed as a consensus belief.

### Historical origin

```json

  "SOURCE_IDS": [
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Adversarial conflation of a modelled controllable event with authority and practical ability to perform the real action.

### Problem it addressed

An adaptation design assumes it may change components, users' choices or physical actuators merely because the model allows it.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

An adaptation design assumes it may change components, users' choices or physical actuators merely because the model allows it.

### Mechanism

Reject self-authorising control; separately establish observation, actual actuation capability, external permission and the expected consequence.

### Trigger or context

A formal or generated controller is proposed for real runtime configuration.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Keep the result advisory or use an existing authorised operational process when actuation is not established.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Model/plant mapping, permissions, controllability and operational feedback.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Dynamic and AI-assisted variants remain bounded by a declared family; broader ecosystem derivatives may require a different organisational model.",

  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Models, controllers, generators and validation assets participate in derivation, but each has a separate realisation and maintenance burden.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Observed runtime state and generated output need identity distinct from the requested configuration or prompt.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Safe endpoints do not establish safe transitions; proofs require adequate plant models and generated outputs require independent validation.",
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: An adaptation design assumes it may change components, users' choices or physical actuators merely because the model allows it. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Operators and accountable product authorities decide deployment; the proof author cannot create authority through notation.

### Actor and authority boundary

Operators and accountable product authorities decide deployment; the proof author cannot create authority through notation. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Model/plant mapping, permissions, controllability and operational feedback.; the result must expose Each real action is feasible and authorised, and the observed consequence is checked separately from the command being issued. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

Each real action is feasible and authorised, and the observed consequence is checked separately from the command being issued.

## ESPLE-061 — Support-aware retirement of variability

Historical source pointer: `/properties/60` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
{
  "FORMULATION": "Industrial legacy management can exclude old variation from new products while retaining artefacts needed for supported products.",
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
    "ESPLE-S007",
    "ESPLE-S011",
    "ESPLE-S041",
    "ESPLE-S045"
  ],
  "DATING_RULE": "Dates identify inspected formulations, not proof that the earliest listed work invented every element.",
  "SOURCE_DATES": {
    "ESPLE-S007": "1999",
    "ESPLE-S011": "2002-06",
    "ESPLE-S041": "2018 conference; 2021-10-12 arXiv deposit",
    "ESPLE-S045": "2026-04-17"
  }
}
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

Industrial legacy management can exclude old variation from new products while retaining artefacts needed for supported products.

### Problem it addressed

Obsolete options burden new derivation, but indiscriminate removal destroys the ability to maintain older variants.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Obsolete options burden new derivation, but indiscriminate removal destroys the ability to maintain older variants.

### Mechanism

Distinguish admission to new products from continued support of existing ones; retire or bind variability when its consumer disappears, preserving required historical baselines and migration paths.

### Trigger or context

A feature is unused for new demand, fixed to one value or becomes disproportionately costly to support.

### Non trigger or cheap path

Mark the feature unavailable to new configurations while retaining the older baseline, rather than immediately rewriting all code.

### Dependencies or preconditions

Actual usage, supported products, external commitments and the effect of removal on derivation and tests.

### Domain profile

```json
{
  "FAMILY_SCOPE_AND_EXPECTED_REUSE": "Evolution preserves or explicitly revises obligations of supported products, not just the newest family model.",
  "COMMONALITY_AND_VARIABILITY_ASSUMPTIONS": "Some variation remains valuable only for legacy support; recovery from products must distinguish history from desired future scope.",
  "FEATURE_SEMANTICS_AND_CONSTRAINTS": "Feature names and model syntax can change without semantic change, and stable names can conceal changed intent.",
  "VARIATION_POINT_AND_BINDING_TIME": "Migration can change binding or traverse intermediate configurations; live transitions require the separate dynamic model.",
  "CORE_ASSET_AND_PRODUCT_RELATION": "Changes may affect code, tests, requirements, generators, interfaces and production plans together.",
  "DERIVATION_AND_CONFIGURATION_IDENTITY": "Old and new product identities and compatibility relations must remain distinguishable during migration or parallel support.",
  "FEATURE_INTERACTION_AND_COVERAGE_BOUNDARY": "Regression work targets affected variants and assumptions; model-set preservation alone does not establish behaviour preservation.",
  "EVOLUTION_AND_MIGRATION_OBLIGATIONS": "Migration, deprecation, test repair and retirement have different consumers and triggers; support-aware retirement need not retain new-product admission.",
  "OWNERSHIP_AND_PRODUCT_EXCEPTION_POLICY": "Core and product maintainers coordinate fixes; a shared change cannot silently override an exceptional product obligation.",
  "ECONOMIC_ASSUMPTIONS_AND_NON_PRODUCT_LINE_ALTERNATIVE": "Retaining multiple baselines has costs, but premature consolidation can break products; cloning or deferred migration can be the adequate alternative.",
  "PROFILE_ID": "ESPLE-DP-P7",
  "PROPERTY_SPECIFIC_APPLICATION": {
    "FAMILY": "P7",
    "TRIGGER": "A feature is unused for new demand, fixed to one value or becomes disproportionately costly to support.",
    "CONSEQUENTIAL_INPUTS": "Actual usage, supported products, external commitments and the effect of removal on derivation and tests.",
    "REALISATION_OR_DECISION": "Distinguish admission to new products from continued support of existing ones; retire or bind variability when its consumer disappears, preserving required historical baselines and migration paths.",
    "ACCEPTANCE_BOUNDARY": "New products no longer inherit unnecessary variation, and supported older products retain an adequate maintenance or migration path.",
    "NON_FAMILY_OR_CHEAPER_ALTERNATIVE": "Mark the feature unavailable to new configurations while retaining the older baseline, rather than immediately rewriting all code.",
    "SUPPORT_AND_RETIREMENT": "End the retirement control itself when no supported product or retained obligation depends on the old variation."
  }
}
```

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Obsolete options burden new derivation, but indiscriminate removal destroys the ability to maintain older variants. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Product support and core maintainers agree retirement; apparent non-use in a sample is not permission to abandon a supported customer.

### Actor and authority boundary

Product support and core maintainers agree retirement; apparent non-use in a sample is not permission to abandon a supported customer. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: Actual usage, supported products, external commitments and the effect of removal on derivation and tests.; the result must expose New products no longer inherit unnecessary variation, and supported older products retain an adequate maintenance or migration path. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

New products no longer inherit unnecessary variation, and supported older products retain an adequate maintenance or migration path.

## ESPLE-062 — Null configurability as context-independent behaviour

Historical source pointer: `/properties/61` in the [exact ledger](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/theislampill/IMPLEMENTAUDIT.md/cacfe97da7656b472bf4f11e50c9d106a6c3f92a/docs/research/genealogy/sss/evolved-software-product-line-engineering/corpus/EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING_PROPERTY_LEDGER.json).

### Lineage id

EVOLVED_SOFTWARE_PRODUCT_LINE_ENGINEERING

### Candidate origin

Source-grounded mechanism or claim registered during this independent study; no target or sibling vocabulary supplied the candidate.

### Historical origin

```json
  "SOURCE_IDS": [
```

### Lineage class

WITHIN_TRADITION_ANALYTICAL_RECONSTRUCTION

### Original form

A 2026 null-variability proposal combines early binding with broad behavioural-consistency wording, while also allowing non-identical outputs for different inputs.

### Problem it addressed

Absence of configuration choices is interpreted as proof that execution is independent of environment or always predictable.

### Failure mode it tries to prevent

Absence of configuration choices is interpreted as proof that execution is independent of environment or always predictable.

### Mechanism

Reject that stronger implication. Retain the narrower distinction that selected features are fixed after a declared binding time; analyse inputs, state, timing and environment separately.

### Trigger or context

Fixed feature selection is used to infer environment-independent behaviour or a general correctness guarantee.

### Non trigger or cheap path

A fixed-function program that reads a changing external value supplies a logical counterexample to the stronger inference.

### Dependencies or preconditions

A clear separation of configuration, ordinary input, internal state and operating environment.

### Domain profile

### Expected engineering payoff

Avoid or reveal this failure without imposing the fuller mechanism where unnecessary: Absence of configuration choices is interpreted as proof that execution is independent of environment or always predictable. No numerical benefit is asserted.

### Decision or consumer

Modellers and assurance readers interpret the claim at its proper level; terminology cannot supply an unproved behavioural theorem.

### Actor and authority boundary

Modellers and assurance readers interpret the claim at its proper level; terminology cannot supply an unproved behavioural theorem. A diagnosis, model result or recommendation is not by itself authorisation, capacity to execute, or evidence that the consequence occurred.

### Communication or interaction requirement

The consequential exchange is the following input and result, between the consumer roles already identified: A clear separation of configuration, ordinary input, internal state and operating environment.; the result must expose The claim is bounded to lack of configurability, without denying ordinary input/output dependence or external failure modes. No separate meeting, team or owner is required merely by this record.

### Observable postcondition

The claim is bounded to lack of configurability, without denying ordinary input/output dependence or external failure modes.
